TL;DR: 本文针对腰椎MRI影像自动报告生成任务，指出标准词汇/语义评估指标无法反映临床诊断准确性，并提出了一个与架构无关的框架，通过半监督U-Net++生成的椎间盘级异常热图增强VLM输入，提升解剖学敏感性和临床可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射科报告撰写耗时且存在阅片者间差异，自动化报告生成是VLM有吸引力的临床应用方向。然而现有评估指标（词汇和语义指标）无法正确反映临床诊断正确性，流畅的报告可能包含临床意义的诊断错误。

Method: 提出了一个架构无关的框架，通过半监督U-Net++模型生成空间定位的椎间盘级异常热图，将这些热图作为VLM输入的增强信息，实现显式的视觉锚定并为临床监督提供独立的可解释性输出。

Result: 标准词汇和语义指标在腰椎MRI VLM报告评估中不能反映临床准确性；所提出的热图增强框架通过显式视觉锚定改善了解剖学敏感性，并提供了独立的可解释性输出用于临床监管。

Conclusion: 该框架朝着诊断可靠、视觉锚定的腰椎MRI解释VLM迈进了重要一步，弥补了现有指标与临床实际需求之间的差距。

Abstract: Radiology reporting is time-consuming and subject to inter-rater variability, making automated report generation an attractive clinical application for Vision-Language Models (VLMs). We benchmark state-of-the-art VLMs on lumbar spine MRI with a focus on diagnostic accuracy and demonstrate that standard lexical and semantic metrics poorly reflect clinical correctness: fluent, well-structured reports can score highly while containing clinically meaningful diagnostic errors. To address this failure mode, we propose an architecture-agnostic framework that augments VLM inputs with spatially localized, disc-level anomaly heatmaps generated by a semi-supervised U-Net++ model. These heatmaps both improve anatomical sensitivity through explicit visual grounding and provide an independent interpretability output for clinical oversight, moving us closer to diagnostically reliable, visually grounded VLMs for lumbar spine MRI interpretation.

</details>


### [55] [Multiple Hypothesis Flow Estimation for Video Frame Interpolation under Matching Ambiguity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07120)
*Zibo Su,Jing Kong,Ruixing Wang,Zhanhe Zhang,Kun Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多假设光流估计框架用于视频帧插值（VFI），在模糊区域保留top-K候选对应关系，并通过可靠性引导的路由器为每个位置选择一个假设，以解决单流估计器在歧义区域丢弃有效候选导致重影和结构失真的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于光流的VFI方法在包含重复/随机纹理、旋转对称结构或快速运动模糊的歧义区域，单个光流估计器只能保留一个位移而丢弃其他合理候选，导致错误匹配和伪影（重影、结构失真、模糊）。

Method: 提出多假设光流估计框架：1) 保留top-K候选对应关系；2) 通过可靠性引导的路由器为每个像素位置选择一个假设；3) 每个假设从粗匹配锚点初始化并通过锚点中心的局部注意力分别细化；4) 帧合成基于选定的单一流-外观假设而非候选运动的软组合。

Result: 在提出的MA-HD基准和公开VFI基准上，该方法的LPIPS和DISTS指标优于对比方法。

Conclusion: 通过保留并选择多个候选光流假设而非仅保留单一流估计，可以有效缓解歧义区域中的匹配不确定性问题，从而改善帧插值的感知质量（LPIPS、DISTS）。

Abstract: Many flow-based video frame interpolation (VFI) methods synthesize an intermediate frame by estimating optical flow fields, warping the two input frames, and blending the warped observations. These latent flow fields are typically learned through image-level reconstruction supervision without direct flow annotations. In ambiguous regions containing repetitive or stochastic textures, rotating symmetric structures, or fast motion with blur, the matching evidence for a single query may contain multiple comparable and spatially separated peaks. Although the ground-truth intermediate frame provides indirect supervision, it may not uniquely identify the latent correspondence in ambiguous regions.When several locations provide multiple plausible matches, a single-flow estimator can retain only one displacement and discard the remaining candidates. If the selected match is incorrect or inconsistent with those of neighboring pixels, warping samples content from mismatched locations, producing ghosting, structural distortion, or blur.To address this limitation, we propose a multiple hypothesis flow estimation framework that preserves top-K candidate correspondences and selects one per location through a reliability-guided router. Each hypothesis is initialized from a coarse matching anchor and refined separately through anchor-centered local attention. Frame synthesis is thus conditioned on one selected flow-appearance hypothesis rather than a soft combination of candidate motions.Experiments on the proposed MA-HD benchmark and public VFI benchmarks show that our method achieves the best LPIPS and DISTS among the compared methods.

</details>


### [56] [Human-AI Perceptual Alignment by Playing Hues and Cues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07141)
*Nuria Alabau-Bosque,Jorge Vila-Tomás,Paula Daudén-Oliver,Pablo Hernández-Cámara,Valero Laparra,Jesús Malo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于桌游Hues and Cues的颜色空间评估框架，通过100个词汇和325名人类观察者的数据建立人类一致性基线，评估162个对比视觉语言模型（CVLM）在语义颜色接地方面的表现。结果显示CVLM能复现人类记忆颜色偏差，但在抽象、主观和流行文化领域系统性偏离人类基线，并发现两种严重错位失败模式，以及高质量精选预训练数据比大规模未精选数据更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基准测试忽视了CVLM与人类感知之间细粒度的语义和文化细微差异，需要一种能够深入评估模型对语义颜色理解的新框架。

Method: 利用桌游Hues and Cues的游戏化离散颜色空间，将480个色格映射至CIE xy色度图，用100个词汇覆盖7个语义类别，通过325名人类观察者的颜色关联数据建立人类一致性基线（LOO交叉验证），评估162个来自不同架构和预训练数据集的模型。

Result: CVLM能复现人类认知偏差（如具体物体的理想化记忆颜色），但在抽象、主观和流行文化领域系统性偏离人类基线；发现两种失败模式：语义误分类和不确定性塌缩为默认蓝色坐标；精选预训练数据比大规模未精选数据更有效地缓解严重错位。

Conclusion: 当前CVLM虽然具备广泛分类能力，但仍无法捕捉人类颜色记忆中细致且局部化的共识，强调游戏化任务在暴露模型潜在偏差方面的价值。

Abstract: Evaluating the perceptual alignment between Contrastive Vision-Language Models (CVLMs) and humans is typically constrained by traditional benchmarks that overlook fine-grained semantic and cultural nuances. In this work, we propose a novel evaluation framework that leverages the gamified, discrete color space of the board game Hues and Cues. By mapping the board's 480 color cells to the CIE xy chromaticity diagram, we calculate empirical perceptual distances across a carefully curated 100-word vocabulary spanning seven semantic categories. To properly contextualize model performance, we establish an empirical lower bound of expected error-the Human Consistency baseline-calculated via Leave-One-Out (LOO) cross-validation on a dense dataset of color associations collected from 325 human observers through a custom digital interface. We evaluate 162 models across multiple architectural families and pre-training datasets to assess their semantic color grounding. Our results demonstrate that while CVLMs successfully replicate human cognitive biases, such as idealized memory colors for concrete physical referents (e.g., food and plants), they systematically diverge from the human baseline in abstract, subjective, and pop-culture domains. We identify two distinct failure modes in severely misaligned concepts: semantic misclassification and a systematic uncertainty collapse into a default blue coordinate. Furthermore, we reveal that highly curated pre-training datasets are significantly more effective than massive, uncurated corpora in mitigating these severe misalignments. Ultimately, this work highlights that despite their broad categorization capabilities, current CVLMs still fail to capture the nuanced, localized consensus of human color memory, emphasizing the value of gamified tasks in exposing underlying model biases. The data and code are publicly available to test other metrics.

</details>


### [57] [InstanceSplat: Instance-Aware Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian Splatting for Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07144)
*Minchao Jiang,Xiaoxuan Ma,Shunyu Jia,Haoru Wang,Zhang Liang,Wentao Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InstanceSplat是一个统一的feed-forward 3D高斯泼溅(3DGS)框架，从无位姿的多视角图像中同时实现通用3D重建和实例感知场景理解。在单次前向传播中，它构建了联合编码外观、几何、实例身份和语言对齐语义的实例感知高斯表示，并通过实例中心学习策略使重建、实例学习和语义学习相互促进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有feed-forward 3DGS方法用于场景理解时大多是类别导向的，而实例感知3DGS方法通常依赖逐场景优化，且将重建与实例/语义学习解耦，缺乏三者间的交互促进。

Method: 1) 提出统一的feed-forward 3DGS框架InstanceSplat，从无位姿多视角图像单次前向传播构建实例感知高斯表示；2) 共享3D高斯体跨视角锚定实例身份，产生可渲染且跨视角一致的实例特征；3) 设计实例中心学习策略，通过共享实例结构连接重建、实例学习和语义学习，其中实例线索引导重建，语言对齐语义增强同类别实例的判别能力，实例区域聚合语义证据为对象级预测。

Result: 在新型视角合成、实例分割和开放词汇语义理解三个任务上，在多种输入视角设置及未见数据集上均取得了最先进(SOTA)性能，同时展现出实用效率和强大的泛化能力。

Conclusion: InstanceSplat证明了通过共享实例结构实现重建与场景理解之间的互惠学习是有效的，统一的feed-forward框架能够在保持高效的同时获得强大的跨数据集泛化能力。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables efficient and generalizable 3D reconstruction, but current feed-forward 3DGS methods for scene understanding remain largely category-oriented. In contrast, instance-aware 3DGS methods typically rely on per-scene optimization and often decouple reconstruction from instance and semantic learning, limiting reciprocal interactions among them. We present InstanceSplat, a unified feed-forward 3DGS framework for generalizable 3D reconstruction and instance-aware scene understanding from pose-free multi-view images. In a single forward pass, InstanceSplat constructs an instance-aware Gaussian representation that jointly encodes appearance, geometry, instance identity, and language-aligned semantics. Shared 3D Gaussians ground instance identities across views, producing renderable and cross-view-consistent instance features. To allow reconstruction and scene understanding to benefit from each other, we further design an instance-centric learning strategy that connects reconstruction, instance learning, and semantic learning through shared instance structure. Specifically, instance cues guide reconstruction, language-aligned semantics strengthen the discrimination of confusing same-category instances, and instance regions aggregate semantic evidence into coherent object-level predictions. Experiments on novel-view synthesis, instance segmentation, and open-vocabulary semantic understanding under varying input-view settings and on an unseen dataset demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, practical efficiency, and strong generalization.

</details>


### [58] [Representation-driven Endoscopic Visual Embedding Alignment for Latent Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07176)
*Francisco Caetano,Tim J. M. Jaspers,Haiko Middeljans,Martijn R. Jong,Rixta A. H. van Eijck van Heslinga,Floor Slooter,Albert J. de Groof,Jacques J. Bergman,Peter H. N. De With,Fons van der Sommen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出REVEAL，这是迄今最大的内窥镜生成式基础模型，基于500万帧的多中心数据集GastroNet-5M训练。与依赖域外先验不同，REVEAL使用直接在内窥镜分布上预训练的编码器对齐扩散潜变量与领域特定视觉特征，既可用于高质量图像生成，也可作为特征提取器，在多个基准上表现优于或媲美EndoViT和Endo-FM等专用分类模型，展现出强大的鲁棒性和结构一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内窥镜领域的生成式基础模型发展受限于两个关键挑战：自然图像与临床图像之间的分布差距，以及训练大规模扩散Transformer的高昂计算成本。虽然表示对齐在通用计算机视觉中已证明能提升效率，但其在内窥镜这一高度专业化图像空间中的作用仍不明确。

Method: REVEAL采用编码器直接在内窥镜图像分布上预训练，将扩散潜变量与领域特定的视觉特征（如精细纹理和复杂解剖结构）进行对齐，从而摆脱对域外先验的依赖。模型在GastroNet-5M这一包含500万帧的多中心数据集上进行训练。

Result: 在图像生成方面，REVEAL能产生高保真图像，并在潜空间编辑（如内绘与外绘）中保持稳健的结构一致性。作为特征提取器，它在多个基准上表现与内窥镜领域基础模型（EndoViT、Endo-FM）相当或更优，并且在现实图像退化条件下展现出强表示鲁棒性。

Conclusion: REVEAL通过降低构建专用临床工具的计算门槛，提供了一个开放、多用途的内窥镜基础模型，可用于条件合成、分割和分布外检测等下游任务，为未来智能消化病学系统奠定基础。

Abstract: Developing foundation generative models for endoscopy is limited by the gap between natural and clinical images and the computational cost of training large Diffusion Transformers. Although representation alignment has improved efficiency in general computer vision, its role within the highly specialized endoscopic image space remains unclear. We introduce REVEAL (Representation-driven Endoscopic Visual Embedding Alignment), the largest generative foundation model for endoscopy to date, trained on GastroNet-5M (GN-5M), a multicenter dataset of 5 million endoscopic frames. Instead of depending on out-of-domain priors, REVEAL employs encoders pretrained directly on the endoscopic distribution to align diffusion latents with domain-specific visual features, preserving fine textures and intricate anatomical structures. Beyond image generation, REVEAL also serves as a powerful feature extractor; in multiple benchmarks, it delivers performance that is competitive with, and in several cases exceeds, endoscopic foundation models such as EndoViT and Endo-FM, specifically tuned for classification tasks, while demonstrating strong representation robustness under realistic imaging corruptions. REVEAL produces high-fidelity images and maintains robust structural coherence in latent-space edits such as inpainting and outpainting. This high-capacity backbone lowers the computational threshold for building specialized clinical tools, offering an open, versatile foundation for conditional synthesis, segmentation, and out-of-distribution detection in future intelligent gastroenterology systems.

</details>


### [59] [Flow-Corrected Shape Optimization: Taming Manifold Drift in High-Dimensional 3D Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07199)
*Emilien Seiler,Nicolas Talabot,Yingxuan You,Federico Stella,Pascal Fua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"优化器-纠正器"（optimizer-corrector）交替框架，解决3D形状生成模型潜空间优化中的"流形漂移"问题，通过将目标最小化与基于流匹配（flow matching）的流形纠正解耦，在不损失表达能力的前提下保证优化结果始终落在有效形状流形上，并在气动阻力减小和物体柔度优化等任务中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D形状优化在深度生成模型的潜空间中进行是计算机辅助工程的基础任务，但梯度优化会使潜向量偏离有效形状流形（称为"流形漂移"）。随着现代3D生成模型潜空间维度越来越高，有效形状占比极小，该问题愈发严重。现有方法（如潜空间正则化、流匹配）要么牺牲表达能力，要么在目标引导与生成保真度之间难以权衡，要么在大型模型上计算不可行。

Method: 提出一个优化器-纠正器框架，交替执行两步操作：(1) 梯度步骤用于目标函数最小化；(2) 引导式流匹配将潜状态拉回有效形状流形。核心创新在于将目标优化与流形纠正确解耦——"自由优化、严格纠正"，从而避免两者之间的固有权衡。

Result: 在多种复杂度的生成先验上验证了有效性，从简单的向量潜空间到大规模架构，覆盖气动阻力减小和物体柔度优化等下游任务，证明该框架既保持几何有效性又不牺牲表达能力，且在现有3D形状模型上计算可行。

Conclusion: 该优化器-纠正器交替框架通过解耦目标优化与流形纠正，有效克服了潜空间形状优化中的流形漂移问题，避免了传统方法的权衡困境，为大规模3D生成模型上的形状优化提供了一条计算可行且保真度高的新路径。

Abstract: Optimizing 3D shapes within the latent spaces of deep generative models is fundamental to computer assisted engineering, yet remains prone to a critical failure mode we term manifold drift: the tendency of gradient-based optimization to move latent vectors away from the manifold of valid shapes. This problem is exacerbated in state-of-the-art 3D shape generative models that operate in increasingly high-dimensional latent spaces where valid shapes occupy a vanishingly small fraction of the full space. Existing mitigation strategies, including latent regularization and flow-matching approaches, either sacrifice expressiveness, demand a difficult trade-off between objective guidance and generative fidelity that remains prone to manifold drift, or are computationally infeasible to scale to modern, large-capacity 3D shape models. We introduce a novel optimizer-corrector framework that alternates between gradient steps for objective minimization and guided flow matching to drive the latent state back to the valid shape manifold. By decoupling objective minimization from flow-based correction, optimizing freely and correcting strictly, this alternating design avoids inherent trade-offs, preserving geometric validity without sacrificing expressiveness while remaining computationally feasible on modern 3D shape models. We demonstrate its effectiveness across generative priors of varying complexity, from simple vector latent spaces to large-scale architectures across a variety of downstream optimization tasks, including aerodynamic drag reduction and object compliance optimization.

</details>


### [60] [CANIS: Generation-Assisted 3D Canonicalization via an Image-Semantic Bridge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07256)
*Kendong Liu,Yuxin Yao,Junhui Hou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CANIS，一种利用冻结的图像到3D生成模型的语义方向先验，实现类别无关的3D物体规范化框架，无需规范化训练或类别模板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统3D规范化方法依赖几何线索，而几何线索往往无法提供语义上有意义的物体方向；3D规范化本质上需要语义层面的方向理解。

Method: CANIS从候选视角渲染输入物体并选择信息量高的视图，利用冻结的图像到3D生成模型生成规范化方向的代理物体；输入物体的稀疏结构化潜码引导代理保持几何形状；通过图像补丁识别语义区域，深度反投影定位输入对应的区域，形成语义锚点来约束几何匹配，最终估计刚体变换实现规范化。

Result: 在合成基准上验证了CANIS及其关键组件的有效性；对部分观测和OmniObject3D的定性结果展示了其对不完整和真实世界扫描的适用性；在任意旋转下提升了3D分类、部件分割和稠密对应等下游任务性能。

Conclusion: CANIS证明生成式模型的语义先验可以有效替代规范化专用训练和类别模板，为3D规范化提供了一种无需类别特定知识的通用方案。

Abstract: Canonicalizing 3D object orientation is fundamental to 3D understanding and analysis. Existing approaches often rely on geometric cues, although 3D canonicalization ultimately requires a semantically meaningful orientation. To address this gap, we propose CANIS, a category-agnostic, generation-assisted framework that introduces the semantic orientation prior of a frozen image-to-3D generative model into 3D canonicalization, without canonicalization-specific training or category-specific templates. Specifically, CANIS first renders the input object from candidate viewpoints, selects an informative view, and generates a proxy in a canonical orientation. During generation, a sparse structural latent encoded from the input guides the proxy to preserve the geometry of an object. CANIS then uses the selected image as a semantic bridge between the input and the proxy. Image patches identify semantic regions on the proxy, and depth back-projection locates the corresponding regions on the input. The resulting semantic anchors constrain geometric matching, from which we estimate the rigid transformation that canonicalizes the input. Experiments on synthetic benchmarks validate CANIS and its key components, while qualitative results on partial observations and OmniObject3D suggest its applicability to incomplete and real-world scans. CANIS also improves downstream 3D classification, part segmentation, and dense correspondence under arbitrary rotations. Project page: https://kenkenzaii.github.io/Canis.

</details>


### [61] [Foundation Models Adaptation for Multi-View Multi-modal Cardiac MRI Segmentation and Direct Ejection Fraction Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07291)
*Sina Amirrajab,Cian M Scannell,Volker Vehof,Michael Bietenbeck,Ali Yilmaz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探索了心脏MRI（CMR）基础模型在多视图、多序列心脏分割和左心室射血分数（LVEF）直接估计任务中的微调与组合效果。在CMR-Multi挑战赛中，微调CineMA模型用于cine和LGE分割，并利用两个冻结的CMR基础模型结合注意力多实例学习进行LVEF回归，取得了良好的验证集结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然基础模型在心脏MRI中展现了强大的迁移能力，但其在异质的多样视图和多序列CMR分析中的有效性仍不明确，因此需要系统探索基础模型的微调与组合策略。

Method: ①微调CineMA模型用于cine和晚期钆增强（LGE）分割，覆盖短轴和长轴视图；②对于LVEF的直接估计，使用两个冻结的CMR基础模型提取嵌入向量，并通过基于注意力的多实例学习进行组合回归。

Result: cine分割在短轴、两腔心和四腔心视图的Dice分数分别为0.862、0.883和0.902；LGE分割在各视图的Dice分数在0.621至0.846之间；LVEF直接回归模型的MAE为4.96个百分点，Pearson相关系数为0.91。

Conclusion: 基础模型可以被有效地微调和组合用于多视图CMR分析，但准确的LGE疤痕分割仍然是一个具有挑战性的任务。

Abstract: Foundation models have shown strong transferability in cardiac MRI (CMR), but their effectiveness for heterogeneous multi-view and multi-sequence CMR analysis remains unclear. In this work, we explore the effectiveness of fine-tuning and combining different CMR foundation models for the Universal Multi-Sequence, Multi-Center and Multi-View CMR Segmentation (CMR-Multi) Challenge. CineMA was fine-tuned for cine and late gadolinium enhancement (LGE) segmentation across short-axis and long-axis views. For direct left-ventricular ejection fraction (LVEF) estimation, we used two recent frozen CMR foundation models to extract embedding vectors that were then combined using attention-based multiple-instance learning for LVEF regression. In the challenge validation set, cine segmentation achieved Dice scores of 0.862, 0.883, and 0.902 for short-axis, two-chamber and four-chamber cine MRI, respectively. LGE segmentation achieved Dice scores between 0.621 and 0.846 across views. The direct LVEF regression model achieved an MAE of 4.96 percentage points and a Pearson correlation of 0.91. These results indicate that foundation models can be effectively adapted and combined for multi-view CMR analysis, while accurate LGE scar segmentation remains a challenging task.

</details>


### [62] [EliSeg: Verified Target Construction for Report-Grounded Abnormality Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07299)
*Chengyi Peng,Haoyu Yang,Meixing Shi,Yuxiang Cai,Yankai Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EliSeg框架，解决报告引导的异常分割任务——即模型需直接从未经筛选的放射学报告中确定目标资格、数量及文本到掩码的对应关系，再生成分割掩码。EliSeg采用"提出-验证-修正"的流程，通过语法约束的Actor生成目标槽和掩码，独立的文本Verifier重建合格发现清单，当两者不一致时选择性重新执行Actor。实验表明EliSeg在MIMIC-CXR-ILS上优于直接分割和提取-分割串联方法，并能有效抑制不合格报告的掩码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有放射学分割方法严重依赖推理前的目标身份或空间提示（如点击、边界框），这相当于提供了隐藏的目标神谕，回避了报告中存在的歧义——包括肯定、否定、先前、不确定或不相关发现，以及多个异常并存的情况。作者希望模型能直接从原始报告中自主判断哪些发现是合格的分割目标、有多少个目标以及每个发现对应的掩码区域，从而更接近真实临床应用场景。

Method: EliSeg采用atcor-verify-revise三段式框架：（1）语法约束的Actor提出目标槽和对应掩码；（2）独立且仅基于文本的Verifier重建完整的合格发现清单；（3）当Actor与Verifier的目标结构不一致时，Revision模块选择性重新执行共享的Actor，以调和两者的分歧。

Result: 在MIMIC-CXR-ILS数据集上，EliSeg一致优于直接分割方法和提取-然后-分割的串联方法，且能有效抑制报告中不合格提及的掩码生成。消融实验验证了验证和修正模块的互补作用；在CheXlocalize数据集上的评估展示了EliSeg能够有效迁移到外部数据集。

Conclusion: EliSeg通过将目标构建与掩码生成整合进同一框架，无需预定义目标身份、发现提示、点或边界框即可完成报告引导的异常分割，验证了"提出-验证-修正"机制在处理报告歧义和多个异常共存场景中的有效性，且具备良好的跨数据集泛化能力。

Abstract: Radiology reports describe clinical observations but do not specify executable segmentation targets. They may contain present, negated, prior,uncertain, or irrelevant findings, while multiple valid abnormalities may coexist. Existing segmentation methods largely bypass this ambiguity by receiving a target identity or spatial prompt before inference, which acts as a hidden target oracle. We study report-grounded abnormality segmentation, where a model must determine target eligibility, cardinality, and finding-to-mask correspondence directly from an unfiltered report before delineating the corresponding regions. We propose \textbf{EliSeg}, an atcor--verify--revise framework that integrates target construction with mask generation. A grammar-constrained Actor proposes target slots and masks, an independent text-only Verifier reconstructs the eligible finding inventory, and Revision selectively re-executes the shared Actor when their target structures disagree. EliSeg requires no predefined target identity, finding prompt, point, or bounding box. Experiments on MIMIC-CXR-ILS show that EliSeg consistently outperforms direct segmentation methods and extract-then-segment cascades across findings, while effectively suppressing masks for ineligible report mentions. Ablation studies confirm the complementary roles of verification and revision, and evaluation on CheXlocalize demonstrates effective transfer of the EliSeg to an external dataset.Code is available at https://github.com/Maybach-dream/EliSeg.

</details>


### [63] [Same Attention, Different Truths: Put Logit-Lens over Visual Attention to Detect and Mitigate LVLM Object Hallucination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07302)
*Zichuan Wang,Songlin Yang,Bo Peng,Zhenchen Tang,Yang Li,Beibei Dong,Jing Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现LVLMs中的物体幻觉并非源于视觉注意力不足，而是源于"模型关注了什么"的问题。通过Logit Lens解码高注意力区域的视觉特征，识别出两种幻觉机制：视觉不确定性（语义相似区域触发）和上下文先验（强共现先验触发），并提出训练无关的Detect-Mitigate框架（含Logit-Lens一致性检查和两种针对性缓解策略）在多个幻觉基准上取得SOTA效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究将LVLM物体幻觉归因于视觉注意力不足，但作者发现真实物体和幻觉物体在高注意力层获得同等强度的视觉注意力，说明核心问题不在于注意力多少，而在于模型注意的内容及其原因，需要重新审视幻觉的机制。

Method: 1) 使用Logit Lens解码高注意力区域视觉特征，区分真实与幻觉物体的可解码性差异；2) 识别两种幻觉机制：视觉不确定性（语义相似区域导致）和上下文先验（强共现先验导致）；3) 提出训练无关的Detect-Mitigate框架：Logit-Lens一致性检查用于检测幻觉，High-Attention Regions Masking（HARM）处理视觉不确定性幻觉，Visual Evidence Enhanced Decoding（VEED）处理上下文先验幻觉。

Result: 在多个幻觉基准数据集上取得了最先进（state-of-the-art）的结果，验证了所提出的检测与缓解框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 物体幻觉的根本原因不是视觉注意力不足，而是模型对视觉特征的解码能力不足以及上下文共现先验的干扰。通过区分两种不同机制并采用针对性缓解策略，可以在无需训练的情况下有效减少幻觉。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) often suffer from object hallucination, generating objects that are absent from the image. Prior work largely attributes this to insufficient visual attention. However, we find that both real and hallucinated objects receive equally strong visual attention in the model's mid-to-late layers, suggesting that the key issue may not be how much the model attends, but what it attends to and why. To this end, we decode the visual features of high-attention regions using Logit Lens, and observe that regions corresponding to real objects can be correctly decoded to the target object tokens, whereas those for hallucinated objects cannot. Building on this, we identify two hallucination mechanisms: (i) visual uncertainty, triggered by semantically similar or confusable regions; masking these regions eliminates the hallucination. (ii) contextual prior, triggered by strong co-occurrence priors; even when the initially attended region is masked, the hallucination persists and attention drifts to other regions. Based on these findings, we propose a simple yet effective training-free Detect-Mitigate framework comprising a Logit-Lens Consistency Check to detect hallucination and targeted remedies: High-Attention Regions Masking (HARM) for visual uncertainty hallucination, and Visual Evidence Enhanced Decoding (VEED) for contextual prior hallucination. Our approach achieves state-of-the-art results on multiple hallucination benchmarks. Code will be available.

</details>


### [64] [H2AL: Hyperbolic Hierarchy-aware Aggregative Learning for Registration-based Few-shot Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07340)
*Jia Wang,Jiaming Cai,Zunying Hu,Zhanjie Wu,Jinyuan Liu,Hua Cheng,Yun Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出H2AL框架，利用双曲空间建模解剖结构的层次性，通过层次感知灌注模块和梯度聚合的联合优化算法，提升基于配准的小样本医学图像分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于配准的小样本医学图像分割方法在欧氏空间进行像素级优化与推理，将解剖结构视为扁平且不连续的整体，忽略了其内在的层次结构，导致伪标签质量下降、模糊区域判别能力减弱，限制了分割性能。

Method: 提出双曲层次感知聚合学习框架H2AL，包含两个关键组件：(1) 双曲层次感知灌注(H2I)模块，利用双曲空间的层次建模能力，通过变换引导的监督双曲对比学习学习层次感知表示，并通过门控灌注块将层次先验注入欧氏空间；(2) 基于梯度聚合的端到端联合优化算法，将配准和分割解码器的梯度（包含语义和层次线索）聚合更新共享编码器，促进跨任务协同学习。

Result: 在两个解剖区域、五种实验设置下的大量实验表明，该方法在配准和分割任务上均展现出有效性和高效性。

Conclusion: H2AL通过双曲空间层次建模与梯度聚合联合优化，有效解决了欧氏空间中对解剖结构层次性忽视的问题，同时提升了配准变形合理性和解剖区域判别能力，代码已公开。

Abstract: Registration-based Few-shot medical image segmentation (RFMIS) aims to generate pseudo-labels for unlabeled images by warping a labeled image through registration. However, existing methods primarily perform pixel-level optimization and inference in Euclidean space, treating anatomical structures as flat and disjoint. This neglect of inherent hierarchies degrades pseudo-label quality and weakens the discrimination of ambiguous regions, limiting the segmentation performance. To overcome this challenge, we propose a Hyperbolic Hierarchy-aware Aggregative Learning framework for RFMIS, termed H2AL, that enhances both deformation plausibility and anatomical discrimination for dual-task learning. Specifically, we introduce a Hyperbolic Hierarchy-aware Infusion (H2I) module, which leverages the hierarchical modeling capability of hyperbolic space to learn precise hierarchy-aware representations via transformation-guided supervised hyperbolic contrastive learning, and injects such hierarchical priors into Euclidean space through a gated infusion block while preserving semantic richness. Furthermore, we propose an end-to-end joint optimization algorithm by gradient aggregation, where the gradients from the registration and segmentation decoders, embedding semantic and hierarchical cues, are aggregated to update the shared encoder to promote collaborative learning across tasks. Extensive experiments on two anatomical regions, with five experimental settings, demonstrate the effectiveness and efficiency of our method in both registration and segmentation. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/JiamingCai469/H2AL.

</details>


### [65] [SkySeaLand: A Wide-Format Satellite Transportation Benchmark with an Ultra-Lightweight Detection Baseline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07382)
*Md. Zahid Hasan Riad,Md Sultanul Islam Ovi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了SkySeaLand卫星图像目标检测数据集（1,307张高分辨率图像，19,101个标注框，涵盖飞机、船只、汽车和船舶四类），并推出SkyDet轻量级基线检测器（1.22M参数，60.5 mAP50），同时系统评估了12种主流检测器在该数据集上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星目标检测面临小目标密集和宽幅场景两大挑战，传统方形缩放会丢失细节信息。现有数据集缺乏对陆地-海洋混合交通场景的覆盖，且缺乏针对宽幅卫星图像的目标检测基准。

Method: 构建SkySeaLand数据集（84.5%图像最长边超过3,836像素，73.1%接近3:1宽高比），提供COCO和YOLO双格式标注；统一划分和评估协议下测试YOLO、RT-DETR、DETR和Faster R-CNN四大类共12种检测器；提出SkyDet锚点自由轻量级检测器作为低资源参考基线。

Result: YOLO和RT-DETR系列取得84.4-88.2 mAP50；SkyDet以1.22M参数、4.90MB模型大小达到60.5 mAP50和24.32 mAP50-95，在Tesla T4上推理延迟13.74ms（72.8 FPS）。

Conclusion: SkySeaLand为陆地-海洋混合交通目标检测提供了紧凑基准数据集；较大参数量的检测器在报告的训练配方下并未获得一致的精度提升；SkyDet作为有文档记录的低资源基线参考，而非SOTA精度主张。

Abstract: Satellite object detection is challenged by small targets and wide-format scenes that lose detail under standard square-input resizing. We introduce SkySeaLand, a public dataset of 1,307 high-resolution satellite images and 19,101 verified bounding boxes across airplane, boat, car, and ship classes in terrestrial and maritime scenes. Native COCO and YOLO annotations are provided. The collection is dominated by large source images and wide scene geometry: 84.5 percent exceed 3,836 pixels on the longest side and 73.1 percent are near a 3:1 aspect ratio. We evaluate twelve detectors from the YOLO, RT-DETR, DETR, and Faster R-CNN families using a common split and COCO metrics. The tested YOLO and RT-DETR variants obtain 84.4--88.2 mAP50, with no consistent accuracy gain from larger parameter counts under the reported model-specific recipes. We also report SkyDet, a 1.22 M parameter anchor-free baseline that obtains 60.5 mAP50 and 24.32 mAP50-95 in a 4.90 MB footprint, with 13.74 ms latency (72.8 FPS) on a Tesla T4. SkySeaLand provides a compact benchmark for mixed land--maritime transportation detection, while SkyDet establishes a documented low-footprint reference rather than a state-of-the-art accuracy claim.

</details>


### [66] [GeoDistill-Refine: Silhouette-First Geometry Distillation for Annotation-Free Spacecraft Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07405)
*Yonglong Zhang,Zongwu Xie,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GeoDistill-Refine两阶段框架，利用离线SAM 3伪掩码蒸馏至轻量级分割网络，通过六种固定提示词投票稳定教师输出，并结合符号距离场、骨架和面积约束精炼学生模型，在SpaceSense-Bench上显著提升Image IoU和Boundary F1，部署模型仅0.263M参数且推理约1.1ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础分割模型可为航天器图像提供无人工标注的监督信号，但其预测随文本提示词变化且包含几何误差，这些误差在蒸馏过程中会被放大，影响最终分割质量。

Method: 两阶段蒸馏框架：(1) 融合六种固定提示词的未加权50%投票结果以稳定SAM 3教师输出；(2) 学生先学习前景轮廓，再通过符号距离场、骨架和面积目标进行精炼；(3) 引入样本级门控机制，基于提示词一致性、有效提示词比例和伪掩码面积合理性降低不可靠伪几何的影响。

Result: 在SpaceSense-Bench HJM lockbox上，Image IoU和Boundary F1分别提升0.0456和0.1380；在SPEED+ Lightbox、Sunlamp及TANGO外部评测中表现出有竞争力的区域重叠以及边界质量或前景精度优势；部署模型TinyUNet仅0.263M参数，RTX 4090上约1.1ms/图。

Conclusion: GeoDistill-Refine有效缓解了伪掩码几何噪声在蒸馏中的放大问题，在保持轻量化部署的同时实现了竞争力强的分割性能，且SAM 3和辅助几何分支仅在训练阶段使用。

Abstract: Foundation segmentation models can provide supervision for spacecraft imagery without manual training masks, but their predictions vary with textual prompts and may contain geometric errors that are amplified during distillation. This paper presents GeoDistill-Refine, a two-stage framework that transfers offline SAM 3 pseudo-masks to a compact segmentation network. Six fixed prompts are fused by an unweighted 50% vote to stabilize the teacher output. The student first learns the foreground silhouette and is then refined with signed-distance-field, skeleton, and area objectives derived from the pseudo-mask. A sample-level gate, computed from prompt agreement, the valid-prompt ratio, and pseudo-mask area plausibility, reduces the influence of unreliable pseudo-geometry. On the SpaceSense-Bench HJM lockbox set, GeoDistill-Refine improves Image IoU and Boundary F1 by 0.0456 and 0.1380, respectively, over a plain pseudo-label student. External evaluations on the SPEED+ Lightbox and Sunlamp domains and on TANGO show competitive regional overlap together with gains in boundary quality or foreground precision. The deployed TinyUNet contains 0.263 M parameters and requires approximately 1.1 ms per image on an RTX 4090; SAM 3 pseudo-mask construction and the auxiliary geometry branches are used only during training.

</details>


### [67] [Addressable Memory for Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07408)
*Xindi Wu,Sven Elflein,James Lucas,Olga Russakovsky,Laura Leal-Taixé,Despoina Paschalidou,Jonathan Lorraine,Aljoša Ošep*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出WorldTrace，一种无需训练的视频世界模型长时视觉记忆框架。研究发现KV缓存超出训练视野后，由于时间RoPE偏移越界导致模型无法可靠寻址存储内容，且RoPE旋转空间中的朴素压缩会破坏记忆。WorldTrace通过给每个摘要槽分配分布内的虚拟位置解决寻址问题，并提出了两种压缩方法：WorldTrace-Field（时序一致性）和WorldTrace-Landmark（场景情节回忆）。同时引入LoopBench基准，实验显示两种方法分别提升15.5%和19.5%的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式视频世界模型依赖KV缓存作为视觉记忆来延续生成，但当生成超出训练范围后，时间RoPE偏移越界导致模型无法通过注意力机制可靠检索存储的视觉信息；同时，在RoPE旋转空间中朴素压缩缓存会因平均不兼容的相位而破坏记忆。

Method: 提出WorldTrace训练免费框架，核心是为每个压缩摘要槽分配一个分布内的虚拟位置以保持记忆可寻址性。包含两种压缩方案：WorldTrace-Field压缩历史以维持时序一致性，WorldTrace-Landmark在检测到的场景转换处存储逐字场景痕迹以支持情节回忆。另引入LoopBench基准来评估压缩缓存能否在长绕行后重建之前访问过的场景。

Result: 在LoopBench上，WorldTrace-Field将时序一致性提升15.5%，WorldTrace-Landmark将情节回忆能力提升19.5%，在不重新训练的情况下延长了视觉持久生成的时间。

Conclusion: WorldTrace有效解决了视频世界模型中长时视觉记忆的寻址与压缩问题，通过虚拟位置分配与场景感知压缩策略，显著增强了模型在长时间跨度上的视觉持久性，且无需额外训练即可部署。

Abstract: We study visual persistence in interactive video world models. These models rely on a Key-Value (KV) cache as a growing visual memory to carry forward previously generated frames. However, we find that models can no longer reliably address stored content once rollouts extend beyond the training horizon, because temporal Rotary Positional Embeddings (RoPE) offsets then fall outside the range seen during training and the model struggles to retrieve the relevant visual information through attention. Moreover, naively compressing the cache in the RoPE-rotated space corrupts memory by averaging together incompatible positional phases. To address this, we propose WorldTrace, a training-free memory framework for long-horizon visual persistence. WorldTrace keeps compressed memory addressable by assigning each summary slot a distinct, in-distribution virtual position. Within this addressable cache, we study two memory compression approaches: WorldTrace-Field compresses history for temporal coherence, while WorldTrace-Landmark stores verbatim scene traces at detected transitions for episodic recall. We further introduce LoopBench, a benchmark evaluating whether a compressed cache can reconstruct a previously visited scene after a long detour. WorldTrace-Field improves temporal consistency by +15.5%, and WorldTrace-Landmark improves episodic recall by +19.5% on LoopBench, extending visually persistent generation without retraining.

</details>


### [68] [UniJEPA: A Unified Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture for Task-Agnostic Visual World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07409)
*An Lanji,Dawei Liu,Jin Li,Haoran Xu,Mei Chen,Yu Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniJEPA是一个统一的自监督世界模型框架，通过单一端到端目标函数联合学习图像级光度预测和视频级时间预测，无需EMA、stop-gradient或预训练编码器即可实现抗坍塌的编码器-预测器训练，并在图像、视频和控制基准上达到或超越任务特定JEPA的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有JEPA方法在设计上是碎片化的：I-JEPA专注于图像内掩码预测，Image World Models学习光度变换，而视频级JEPA预测未来时间状态。这些目标被当作不同配方，使用独立的编码器、预测器和抗坍塌正则器，阻碍了单一模型同时统一图像级和视频级世界建模的能力。

Method: UniJEPA提出一个统一的JEPA框架，在共享潜空间中联合学习光度预测（图像级变换）和时间预测（视频级下一状态动态）。采用单一端到端目标，由下一嵌入预测损失和高斯正则器组成，可从原始像素端到端训练，无需EMA、stop-gradient或预训练编码器。训练后通过动作条件后训练，利用目标特征作为预测目标实现零样本规划。

Result: 在图像、视频和控制基准上，UniJEPA匹配或超越了任务特定的JEPA方法，且仅需一个损失超参数。与生成式世界模型相比，在相当精度下规划速度快数十倍。同一潜空间同时支持可控抽象：光度预测学习不变结构，时间预测学习等变动态。

Conclusion: UniJEPA证明了图像级和视频级世界建模可以在单一JEPA框架和共享潜空间中统一实现，通过可证明抗坍塌的编码器-预测器设计，在简化目标函数的同时实现了与任务特定方法相当或更优的性能和高效规划能力。

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) have emerged as a principled framework for self-supervised learning of world models in compact latent spaces, yet existing methods are fragmented: some predict masked parts of a single image in latent space (I-JEPA), others learn to predict global photometric transformations (Image World Models), while video-scale JEPAs predict future temporal states and are post-trained for action-conditioned planning (V-JEPA~2, DINO-World, DINO-WM). These objectives are treated as distinct recipes with separate encoders, predictors, and anti-collapse regularizers, hindering a single model from unifying image-level and video-level world modeling. We present UniJEPA, a unified JEPA that jointly learns photometric prediction (image-level transformations) and temporal prediction (video-level next-state dynamics) in one shared latent space. A single end-to-end objective, composed of a next-embedding prediction loss and a Gaussian regularizer, yields a provably anti-collapse encoder-predictor pair trainable from raw pixels without EMA, stop-gradient, or pre-trained encoders. We show that the same latent space supports controllable abstraction: photometric prediction learns invariant structure while temporal prediction learns equivariant dynamics. After action-conditioned post-training on offline trajectories, UniJEPA enables zero-shot planning by treating goal features as prediction targets. On image, video, and control benchmarks, UniJEPA matches or surpasses task-specific JEPAs while requiring a single loss hyperparameter, and plans up to tens of times faster than generative world models at comparable accuracy.

</details>


### [69] [I Seek You in Videos: Identity-Conditioned Queries for Person-Centric Video Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07417)
*Shibo Gao,Chongxiao Wang,Chenglong Huang,Jie Ma,Haolin Shi,Fei Ding,Jing Li,Qiang Lyu,Yangyang Liu,Yang Liu,Jun Liu,Linlin Huang,Peipei Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出身份条件查询（ICQ）任务，要求模型联合关联并理解输入视频与人物参考图像，并构建了包括基准（ISYV-Bench，1,377个视频/QA对，六个难度层级）、大规模训练集（ISYV-75K，75K样本）和模型框架（ISYV-Framework）在内的系统性解决方案ISYV。实验表明主流MLLM在该基准上表现挣扎，而ISYV-Model优于强基线并接近闭源模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频推理任务通常假设简化的视频-文本设置，忽略了现实世界中多模态、多源输入的特点，导致身份匹配和以人为中心的推理能力受限。为弥合这一差距，作者引入了ICQ任务。

Method: 提出ISYV系统方案，包含三部分：（1）ISYV-Bench评估基准，含1,377个真实复杂视频和QA对，按六个难度层级组织；（2）ISYV-75K大规模训练集，通过自动标注、多阶段验证和人工审核构建；（3）ISYV-Framework，包含面向ICQ的模型与训练策略，无需额外镜头级标注即可利用信息性视频镜头。

Result: 主流闭源和开源MLLM在ISYV-Bench上均表现困难，尤其在跨域身份匹配和长时序跟踪方面。ISYV-Model优于强基线，在某些方面接近闭源模型性能。

Conclusion: ISYV提供了统一的任务定义、可扩展的数据集/基准以及面向以人为中心视频推理的建模见解，填补了现实视频推理中身份条件多模态理解的空白。

Abstract: Real-world video reasoning often involves multimodal, multi-source inputs, whereas existing video reasoning tasks typically assume a simplified video-text setting, limiting identity matching and person-centric reasoning. To bridge this gap, we introduce the Identity-conditioned Queries (ICQ) task, in which models are required to jointly associate and interpret an input video and a reference image of a person, and leverage this conditioning to address identity grounding, behavior understanding, and temporal reasoning, among other challenges. Building on ICQ, we present ISYV (I Seek You in Videos), a systematic solution comprising three components: (1) ISYV-Bench, a challenging evaluation benchmark with 1,377 real-world complex videos and 1,377 question-answer pairs, organized into six difficulty levels spanning capabilities from identity recognition to causal reasoning; (2) ISYV-75K, a large-scale training set of 75K high-quality samples constructed via automated annotation, multi-stage verification, and manual review; and (3) ISYV-Framework, containing an ICQ-oriented model and training strategy for learning to exploit informative video shots without additional shot-level annotations. Extensive experiments show that both mainstream closed-source and open-source MLLMs struggle on ISYV-Bench, especially in cross-domain identity matching and long-horizon tracking. ISYV-Model outperforms strong baselines and in some aspects approaches closed-source performance. Overall, ISYV provides a unified task definition, scalable datasets/benchmarks, and modeling insights for person-centric video reasoning.

</details>


### [70] [Conformal Coverage Guarantees for Any Video Temporal Grounder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07434)
*Aseel Mohamed,Rasul Khanbayov,Erchin Serpedin,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: COVER是一个与模型无关的后处理包装器，可将任何视频时序定位器转换为输出包含真实时刻的时序区域，保证概率至少为1-α。它通过校准时序非一致性分数的分位数来扩大基础预测范围，无需重新训练或白盒访问。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频事件边界存在模糊性：同一查询-视频对被不同标注者重标注时，超过一半样本的标记区间重叠度不到50%。地面真实值本质上是区间上的分布，但现有定位器仅返回单一区间且不提供可靠性声明，导致部署时错误区间与正确区间无法区分。

Method: COVER作为后处理包装器，采用时序非一致性分数校准分位数，将基础预测区间扩大相应量。提出了两类分数：用于输出区间的两侧边界扩大分数，以及用于输出相关性信号的超水平集分数。开发了针对时序定位的特定理论，包括认证区域大小的界、条件覆盖事件长度的保证，以及可交换性被破坏时的退化分析。

Result: 在三个基准测试和五个定位器上，实际覆盖率跟踪目标覆盖率，校准过程揭示了点指标所隐藏的信息。

Conclusion: COVER在有限样本和分布无关（可交换性条件下）提供了覆盖率保证，无需重新训练或白盒访问，可将任何定位器（训练好的定位器或黑盒视频-语言模型）转化为输出带可靠性保证的时序区域。

Abstract: Event boundaries in continuous video are ambiguous: re-annotate the same query-video pair and independent annotators mark moments that overlap by less than half on a large fraction of samples. The ground truth for video temporal grounding is therefore a distribution over intervals, yet every grounder returns a single interval with no statement of reliability, so at deployment a wrong interval is indistinguishable from a right one. COVER changes the output object: a post-hoc, model-agnostic wrapper that turns any grounder, a trained localizer or a black-box video--language model, into one that emits a temporal region containing the true moment with probability at least $1-α$, by calibrating the quantile of a temporal nonconformity score on held-out labels and widening the base prediction by that amount. The guarantee is finite-sample and distribution-free under exchangeability, and requires neither retraining nor white-box access. We give two score families, a two-sided boundary-widening score for grounders that emit an interval and a super-level-set score for grounders that emit a relevance signal, and develop theory specific to grounding that bounds how large the certified region becomes, when coverage survives conditioning on event length, and how it degrades when moments from one video break exchangeability. Across three benchmarks and five grounders, realized coverage tracks the target, and calibration exposes what point metrics hide.

</details>


### [71] [SABRE: Scalable and Automated Benchmarking of VLMs under Stress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07435)
*Zixuan Lan,Luzhe Sun,Matthew R. Walter,Jiawei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SABRE，一个可扩展的自动化流水线，用于构建视觉语言模型（VLM）的压力测试。该框架将测试设计（Markdown Task Design与Data Schema）转化为结构化规格、生成或编辑的图像和问答对，并通过自动化过滤和人工审查保证质量。作者实例化为SABRE-Prior，测试VLM是否遵循视觉证据而非依赖世界先验，结果表明在六个VLM上宏平均准确率仅17.8%-31.3%，证明现有VLM在面对反直觉视觉场景时表现薄弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（VLM）发展迅速，但基准测试开发滞后，难以识别模型弱点。构建压力测试成本高昂——样本必须满足受控条件、保持可回答性、并挑战当前模型。亟需一个可扩展、自动化的压力测试构建框架。

Method: SABRE是一种可扩展的自动化流水线，将测试蓝图（Markdown任务设计与数据模式）转换为结构化规格说明、生成或编辑的图像以及问答对。自动化过滤剔除被过滤VLM解决的候选样本，人工审查验证候选有效性、支持注释修正和局部图像修复。实例化SABRE-Prior，涵盖四种测试类型：Context（熟悉场景中的意外实体）、Texture（反事实材质）、Attribute（非规范组件数量）、Language Elicitation（语言暗示但图像不支持的回答）。

Result: SABRE-Prior包含600张图像和1,000个问题。在六个VLM上，宏平均准确率范围为17.8%至31.3%，平均22.6%。真实图像的Attribute对照组对过滤VLM同样困难。SABRE-Counting和SABRE-Spatial试点实验表明该工作流可支持其他压力测试设置。

Conclusion: SABRE被确立为一个可复用的VLM压力测试构建与刷新框架，而非单一固定的基准测试。实验结果揭示现有VLM在需要遵循视觉证据而非世界先验的任务中表现显著不足，验证了该框架的有效性和通用性。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are improving rapidly, but benchmark development lags behind, making weaknesses hard to identify. Building stress tests is costly: samples must satisfy controlled conditions, remain answerable, and challenge current models. We present SABRE, a scalable, automated pipeline that converts a Test Primer (a Markdown Task Design with Data Schema) into structured specifications, generated or edited images, and question-answer pairs. Automated filtering removes candidates solved by a Filtering VLM, while human review verifies candidate validity and supports annotation correction and localized image repair. We instantiate SABRE-Prior to test whether VLMs follow visual evidence instead of relying on world priors -- learned expectations about familiar objects and scenes. Its 600 images and 1,000 questions span Context (unexpected entities in familiar scenes), Texture (counterfactual materials), Attribute (noncanonical component counts), and Language Elicitation (answers suggested by language but unsupported by the image). Across six VLMs, macro-average accuracy ranges from 17.8% to 31.3% (22.6% mean). A real-image Attribute control is comparably difficult for the Filtering VLM. SABRE-Counting and SABRE-Spatial pilots show that the workflow supports other stress-test settings. These results establish SABRE as a reusable framework for constructing and refreshing VLM stress tests rather than a single fixed benchmark.

</details>


### [72] [MirrorWorld: Taming Video Diffusion Models for Mirror Reflection Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07463)
*Youjun Zhao,Alex Warren,Gary K. L. Tam,Rynson W. H. Lau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MirrorWorld，一种反射感知的视频修复框架，通过语义关系蒸馏（SRD）和几何变换对齐（GTA）两个互补组件来建模场景与镜面之间的关系，实现高质量的镜面反射视频生成，并构建了统一的视频镜面反射基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散模型在高质量视频合成方面取得显著进展，但镜面反射的生成仍然具有挑战性，因为镜面内容必须与周围场景保持一致。现有的视频扩散模型并未专门设计来建模场景到镜面的关系，导致生成的反射可能出现内容错误或空间排列不一致的问题。

Method: 提出MirrorWorld框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 语义关系蒸馏（SRD），从冻结的视觉基础模型中转移关系信息，鼓励可见场景内容与镜面区域之间的语义关联，建模"应该反射什么"；(2) 几何变换对齐（GTA），学习引导反射内容空间排列的变换，建模"应该如何排列"。此外，将四个现有视频镜面数据集重新整理为统一的反射重建任务基准。

Result: 实验结果表明，MirrorWorld在反射重建质量上优于代表性的基于图像的反射生成方法和强视频修复基线，验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: MirrorWorld通过将反射生成分解为语义内容和空间排列两个互补的挑战，分别用SRD和GTA进行建模，有效解决了视频镜面反射生成中场景到镜面关系建模的难题，并为该领域提供了统一的基准测试平台。

Abstract: Recent advances in video diffusion models (VDMs) have enabled high-fidelity video synthesis. However, generating mirror reflections remains challenging because the content within a mirror must remain consistent with the surrounding scene. Existing VDMs are not specifically designed to model scene-to-mirror relationships, which can lead to reflections with incorrect content or inconsistent spatial arrangements. We observe that mirror reflection generation involves two complementary challenges: determining what scene content should be reflected and how the reflected content should be spatially arranged within the mirror region. Motivated by this observation, we propose MirrorWorld, a reflection-aware video inpainting framework that models scene-to-mirror relationships during generation. Specifically, we introduce Semantic Relation Distillation (SRD), which transfers relational information from a frozen visual foundation model to encourage semantic associations between visible scene content and mirror regions. We further propose Geometric Transformation Alignment (GTA), which learns a transformation that guides the spatial arrangement of reflected content. The two components play complementary roles, with SRD modeling what should be reflected and GTA modeling how it should be arranged. To facilitate research on this problem, we construct a benchmark for video mirror reflection generation by repurposing four existing video mirror datasets into a unified reflection reconstruction task. Experimental results show that MirrorWorld achieves improved reflection reconstruction quality over representative image-based reflection generation methods and strong video inpainting baselines.

</details>


### [73] [SimWAM: A Simple World Action Model for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07468)
*Zongchuang Zhao,Xin Zhou,Tianyang Xu,Zhengyang Sun,Kaixuan Zhou,Honglin Li,Dingkang Liang,Xiang Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SimWAM是一种简单而有效的世界动作模型，利用视频生成作为纯训练信号，通过联合流匹配协同训练预训练视频专家和轻量级动作专家，训练后丢弃视频分支，留下独立的自包含规划器直接预测轨迹，在NAVSIM上达到91.5 PDMS并显著降低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WAM方法在推理时需要昂贵的未来帧生成，限制了端到端自动驾驶的实时性，需要一种将视频生成仅用于训练、推理时无需生成的方法。

Method: 1) 将视频生成纯作为训练信号，联合流匹配协同训练视频专家和动作专家；2) 采用隔离注意力掩码使动作预测独立于未来帧；3) 训练后丢弃视频分支，保留自包含规划器直接预测轨迹；4) 通过统一注意力接口使视频骨干可替换、动作专家可独立扩展；5) 应用强化学习优化组合驾驶奖励。

Result: 在NAVSIM上达到91.5 PDMS，超越现有最优WAM规划器且延迟更低，并可零样本迁移到nuScenes数据集。

Conclusion: SimWAM作为简单而可靠的基线，能有效利用视频生成领域的进展，为高效的自动驾驶提供了一种低延迟、可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: World-Action Models (WAMs) improve end-to-end autonomous driving by transferring video dynamics priors to action prediction, but existing methods require costly future generation at inference. We present SimWAM, a simple yet effective WAM that uses video generation purely as a training signal. It co-trains a pretrained video expert and a lightweight action expert with joint flow matching. An isolated attention mask keeps action prediction independent of future frames, allowing the video branch to be discarded after training and leaving a self-contained planner that directly predicts trajectories. Since the two experts share no parameters and interact only through a unified attention interface, the video backbone could be replaced and the action expert scaled independently without modifying the learning objective or inference pipeline. We further apply reinforcement learning to optimize a compositional driving reward beyond trajectory imitation. Our SimWAM achieves $91.5$ PDMS on NAVSIM, surpasses state-of-the-art WAM-based planners with substantially lower latency, and transfers zero-shot to nuScenes. These results position SimWAM as a simple yet solid baseline that could readily benefit from advances in video generation for efficient autonomous driving. The code and model weights are available at https://github.com/H-EmbodVis/SimWAM/

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

### [74] [Can MLLMs Decode the Creative Leap? Introducing C4 for Cross-Concept Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06501)
*Ming Wang,Yuqing Zhang,Tingna Xie,Xiangju Li,Xiaocui Yang,Daling Wang,Shi Feng,Yifei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出C4框架，一个基于认知科学的成语跨概念创造力评估框架，包含编码（项目构建）和解码（模型推理）两个组件，并构建了C4-Eval评测集（184个合成项目+37个人类创作项目，共884个答案恢复任务）。在10个MLLM上的评测显示，最强的闭源模型准确率仅为50.7%和48.0%，开源模型则显著更低，揭示了当前多模态大语言模型在跨概念创造性解码能力上的巨大差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的创造力在设计、沟通、教育和人机协作中至关重要，但现有的评估多聚焦于准确性任务，缺乏对创造性能力的系统评测。跨概念理解是接受性创造力的核心认知能力，但目前缺乏基于明确结构和精确答案的评测框架。

Method: 1. 将项目构建操作化为跨概念编码、模型推理操作化为跨概念解码；2. 构建C4框架，通过手动标注并经第三方审核的跨概念网络，将目标槽映射到可具象化的替代概念，沿桥路径生成批量项目；3. 以桥接数量和深度作为难度指标；4. 构建C4-Eval评测集，包含合成项目和人类创作项目，每个项目在五种任务设置下实例化；5. 在10个MLLM上进行系统评测。

Result: 最强的闭源模型（如GPT等）在主要答案恢复任务上分别达到50.7%和48.0%的准确率，开源模型表现显著更低；候选约束能大幅提升准确率，但桥接提示和解释请求仅带来小幅提升。

Conclusion: 当前MLLM在通过跨概念关系解码创造性编码意义方面存在显著能力差距，需要进一步研究以提升多模态模型的创造性理解能力。C4框架及C4-Eval评测集为这一方向提供了可复现的评估基础。

Abstract: Creative capabilities of MLLMs matter in design, communication, education, and human--AI collaboration, yet remain difficult to evaluate because explicit targets and reward signals are scarce compared with accuracy-oriented tasks. Cross-concept understanding is a core cognitive capacity underlying receptive creativity. It enables a perceiver to recover intended meaning from non-obvious but meaningful conceptual relations. We operationalize item construction as cross-concept encoding and model inference as cross-concept decoding. We introduce C4, a cognition-inspired evaluation framework for Chengyu (Chinese idiom)-based Cross-Concept Creativity. Its encoding component maps target slots to imageable substitute concepts along bridge paths in a manually annotated and third-party-reviewed cross-concept network, enabling batch generation with explicit structure, difficulty indexed by bridge count and depth, and exact answers. Using this framework, we instantiate the C4 Evaluation Set (C4-Eval), comprising 184 synthetic items and 37 human-created cross-concept chengyu figures collected from online sources. We manually construct and review cross-concept relations, bridge paths, and reasoning processes for the collected figures. Each C4-Eval item is instantiated in five task settings, yielding 884 primary answer-recovery cases. Across ten evaluated MLLMs, the strongest closed models reach 50.7% and 48.0% primary accuracy, while open-source models remain substantially lower. Candidate constraints improve accuracy sharply, but bridge hints and explanation requests provide only modest gains. These results expose a substantial gap in how current MLLMs decode creatively encoded meaning through cross-concept relations. The code is in the supplementary material.

</details>


### [75] [From Cheap Fakes to Pure Synthesis: Addressing the New Era of T2V Fake News Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06732)
*Yifeng Luo,Yupeng Li,Liang Lan,Tian Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对文本生成视频（T2V）模型带来的新型纯合成假新闻视频检测问题，将其构建为三元分类任务（真实、廉价伪造、纯合成伪造），并构建了首个纯合成假新闻视频数据集PS-FNVD，同时提出基于推理引导的R-T2V框架，在10个基准方法上取得了最优性能，准确率比第二名高出12.20个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2V生成模型可以凭空合成假新闻视频，超出了传统基于现有素材拼凑的"廉价伪造"范畴，导致现有检测器面临模态对齐陷阱。已有数据集缺乏纯合成假新闻视频样本，而直接使用T2V模型生成完美对齐的样本会导致检测退化为单模态捷径并引起语义-视觉退化。

Method: 将T2V-FNVD建模为三元分类任务（真实/廉价伪造/纯合成伪造），构建PS-FNVD数据集，其中包含两类样本：对齐欺骗的编造事件（Type 1）和真实事件但视觉来源虚假（Type 2），以防止模型利用单模态捷径。在此基础上提出R-T2V框架，通过条件化推理生成和监督微调训练，融合高层语义逻辑与低层物理生成痕迹来预测三元真实性标签。

Result: 在10个主流基线方法上进行大量实验，R-T2V取得了最先进性能，准确率比第二名基线高出12.20个百分点，宏F1分数高出8.46个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过将纯合成假新闻视频作为独立类别来处理，并构建防止单模态捷径的数据集，同时结合语义逻辑与生成痕迹的推理引导框架，能够有效应对T2V模型带来的新型假新闻视频检测挑战。

Abstract: Recent text-to-video (T2V) generation models enable fake news videos to be synthesized from scratch, shifting the threat beyond cheap fakes assembled from existing footage. Such news videos can closely match fabricated narratives, creating a modality alignment trap for existing detectors. Existing datasets lack pure synthesis fake news videos. Although directly prompting T2V models with descriptions of fake news videos can yield perfectly aligned samples, it reduces the fake news video detection (FNVD) to unimodal shortcuts and causes semantic-visual degeneration. To counter this, we formulate T2V-FNVD as a novel ternary classification task with three labels (real, cheap fake, and pure synthesis fake) and construct the first pure synthesis fake news video dataset (PS-FNVD). PS-FNVD includes fabricated events with aligned deception (Type 1) and true events with false visual provenance (Type 2), preventing models from exploiting unimodal shortcuts. Furthermore, we propose the Reasoning-guided T2V-FNVD (R-T2V) framework. Trained through conditioned rationale generation and supervised fine-tuning, R-T2V integrates high-level semantic logic with low-level physical generative traces to predict the ternary veracity label. Extensive experiments across 10 prevailing baselines show that R-T2V achieves the state-of-the-art performance, outperforming the second-best baseline by 12.20 percentage points in accuracy and 8.46 percentage points in macro $F_1$.

</details>


### [76] [DocMemo: Dynamic Evidence Discovery via Probabilistic Memory-Guided Retrieval for Multi-Modal Document Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07067)
*Hanshu Yao,Janfeng Zhong,Niu Lian,Jinpeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DocMemo提出了一种基于记忆引导的框架，将长文档推理建模为动态证据探索过程，通过三层检索记忆（文档结构记忆、页面信念记忆、问题情节记忆）结合贝叶斯页面信念更新与汤普森采样，实现跨轮次的动态页面选择与细粒度证据访问，在3个基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长文档理解系统存在两大局限：单轮方法固定top-k页面集合，无法从早期检索错误中恢复；迭代方法虽支持多轮证据获取，但未探究跨轮次状态的传播机制，难以追踪页面相关性的动态变化。

Method: DocMemo维护三层检索状态：文档结构记忆（捕获结构先验）、页面信念记忆（动态相关估计）、问题情节记忆（查询特定推理轨迹）。通过贝叶斯页面信念更新结合汤普森采样、空间邻近传播和结构感知的自适应粒度证据访问来持续优化跨轮次页面选择，并以细粒度视觉区域补充页面级证据。

Result: 在3个基准上达到最先进性能，实验验证了结构化记忆和动态页面信念更新的有效性。

Conclusion: DocMemo通过记忆引导的动态证据探索框架有效解决了长文档理解中静态检索的局限和跨轮次状态传播的困难，证明了结构化记忆与动态信念更新在长文档推理中的价值。

Abstract: Long-document understanding requires locating sparse and heterogeneous evidence across hundreds of pages, yet existing systems remain limited by static retrieval and fragile cross-round memory. Mainstream single-round methods commit to a fixed top-$k$ page set at the outset and struggle to recover from early retrieval errors; recent iterative approaches allow multi-round evidence acquisition, but they do not investigate the propagation mechanism of cross-round states, making it difficult to track the dynamic changes in page relevance. To address these limitations, we propose DocMemo, a memory-guided framework that formulates long-document reasoning as dynamic evidence exploration. DocMemo maintains a tri-level retrieval state consisting of Document Schema Memory, Page Belief Memory, and Question Episodic Memory, which respectively capture structural priors, dynamic relevance estimation, and query-specific reasoning trajectories. During reasoning, DocMemo continuously refines cross-round page selection through Bayesian page belief updating with Thompson sampling, spatial proximity propagation, and structure-aware adaptive-granularity evidence access, while supplementing page-level evidence with fine-grained visual regions. Experiments on 3 benchmarks show that DocMemo achieves state-of-the-art performance and validate the efficacy of structured memory and dynamic page belief updating. Code is available at https://github.com/Harrygof/DocMemo.

</details>


### [77] [Towards Multi-Label Graph Foundation Models: from Single-Vector Representation Learning to Multi-Semantic Basis Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06394)
*Dongxiao He,Jiayu Zhang,Jitao Zhao,Yi Wang,Di Jin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MSB-GFM，一个用于跨域多标签节点分类的图基础模型框架，通过多语义基表示学习范式将每个多标签节点建模为语义基的自适应组合，并采用语义-结构双通道架构配合域对抗训练实现有效的跨域知识迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在多标签节点分类上虽然能有效建模多个标签，但仅局限于同域场景，跨域泛化能力有限。此外，现有图基础模型（GFMs）基于单标签假设构建，将所有节点都视为只含单一语义类并嵌入到单一表示中，对于多标签节点，这种表示本质上是用表示空间中的单个点去逼近多个语义，导致语义纠缠，难以同时区分多个标签。

Method: 提出MSB-GFM框架，包含两个核心设计：(1) 多语义基表示学习范式，将每个多标签节点建模为语义基的自适应组合，增强对多种语义的灵活表示能力；(2) 语义-结构双通道架构，结合域对抗训练实现有效的跨域知识迁移。

Result: 大量实验验证了模型的有效性，表明MSB-GFM在跨域多标签节点分类任务上表现优异。

Conclusion: MSB-GFM通过多语义基表示学习和双通道域对抗训练，有效解决了现有GFMs在跨域多标签节点分类中的语义纠缠问题，提升了跨域泛化能力。

Abstract: Multi-label node classification is an important yet challenging task in graph learning, where nodes exhibit multiple semantics simultaneously. Existing methods for multi-label node classification can effectively model multiple labels, while only considering in-domain scenarios where the model needs to be trained and tested within the same graph domain, resulting in limited cross-domain generalization. Recently, Graph Foundation Models (GFMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for learning transferable graph representations across diverse graph domains and downstream tasks. However, existing GFMs are built upon single-label assumption, where all nodes are arbitrarily regarded as containing only one class of semantic and embedded into a single representation. For multi-label nodes, such a representation essentially approximates multiple semantics with a single point in the representation space, inevitably leading to semantic entanglement and making simultaneous discrimination of multiple labels difficult. To address these limitations, we propose a Multi-Semantic Basis Graph Foundation Model (MSB-GFM), a framework for cross-domain multi-label node classification. Specifically, we introduce a multi-semantic basis representation learning paradigm that models each multi-label node as an adaptive composition of semantic bases, thereby enabling flexible representational capacity for modeling multiple semantics. Furthermore, we develop a semantic-structure dual-channel architecture with domain adversarial training for effective cross-domain knowledge transfer. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our model.

</details>


### [78] [EntropyMoE: Entropy-Aware Sparse Expert Routing for Tokenizer-Free LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06398)
*Bo Liu,Muxuab Yu,Yu Zhang,Pengfei Gao,Yongping Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EntropyMoE提出了一种基于字节级动态补丁的混合专家架构，利用补丁熵作为路由信号来组织稀疏计算，在保持下游任务精度的同时实现更低的bits-per-byte。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的字节级大语言模型尽管通过动态分块提升了性能，但仍对所有补丁使用相同的密集前馈计算，无法根据补丁语义和粒度的差异自适应调整模型容量。

Method: EntropyMoE将全局补丁Transformer中的密集前馈模块替换为Top-K专家层。每个动态补丁作为专家路由的基本单元，字节覆盖范围决定其工作负载贡献；路由器直接基于补丁熵选择专家，补丁熵与长度共同构成特征空间来调节专家专业化。

Result: 实验表明EntropyMoE在匹配的稠密和稀疏基线上取得了最低的held-out bits-per-byte，同时保持相当的下游任务精度。

Conclusion: 研究证明了补丁熵是稀疏条件计算的有效路由坐标，并将混合专家建模从基于tokenizer的表示扩展到字节级表示。

Abstract: Recent byte-level large language models (LLMs) have made tokenizer-free modeling increasingly competitive by grouping bytes into dynamically sized patches. However, existing byte-patch architectures still apply the same dense feed-forward computation to every patch. This uniform computation cannot adapt model capacity to variations in patch semantics and granularity. We address this limitation with EntropyMoE, a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture designed for dynamic byte patches. EntropyMoE replaces the dense feed-forward modules in the global patch Transformer with Top-K expert layers. Each dynamic patch serves as the basic unit of expert routing, and its byte coverage determines its contribution to workload accounting. The router selects experts directly from patch entropy, using the same granularity signal that underlies dynamic patch construction to organize sparse computation. Patch entropy and length jointly define the feature space for regulating expert specialization. Experiments show that EntropyMoE achieves the lowest held-out bits-per-byte among matched dense and sparse baselines while maintaining comparable downstream accuracy. These results establish patch entropy as an effective routing coordinate for sparse conditional computation and extend Mixture-of-Experts modeling beyond tokenizer-based representations.

</details>


### [79] [Beyond Routing Weights: Faithful Response-Level Interpretation of Mixture-of-Experts Reward Models via Contribution Contrast](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06400)
*Yifan Wang,Jinyi Mu,Mayank Jobanputra,Yu Wang,Soyoung Oh,Isabel Valera,Vera Demberg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CoCo（Contribution-Contrast）方法，用于解释稀疏MoE奖励模型中专家的行为。与现有的基于路由权重的方法不同，CoCo利用选择-拒绝响应对中贡献差异最大的样本构建专家解释，同时捕获路由和偏好判断行为，在自动和人工评估中均优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏MoE奖励模型的解释方法仅依赖于路由权重，只能说明专家接收了哪些提示，无法揭示专家如何评判响应。这种简化不能完整刻画专家的行为，需要一种能够同时捕获路由行为与偏好判断行为的解释方法。

Method: CoCo方法通过计算每个专家在选择-拒绝响应对中的贡献对比度，选取贡献差异最大的样本来构建专家解释，以联合建模路由和偏好行为。通过这种方式，CoCo能够更准确地反映出专家在奖励判断中的真正作用。

Result: 在自动和人工评估中，CoCo生成的专家解释比基于路由器、基于分数和基于稀疏自动编码器的替代方法更连贯、更忠实、更专业化，同时保持了有竞争力的奖励建模准确性。这是MoE奖励模型解释方法的首次系统性研究。

Conclusion: CoCo提供了一种更全面的MoE奖励模型专家解释框架，弥补了纯路由权重方法的不足，为理解奖励模型中专家角色提供了更可靠的途径。

Abstract: Reward models are central to learning from human preferences, yet identifying what drives their predictions remains challenging. Recent sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) reward models seek to improve interpretability by routing prompts to specialized experts and characterizing experts through examples with high routing weights. However, routing weights only reveal which prompts an expert $\textit{receives}$, not how it $\textit{judges}$ responses, providing only a partial account of expert behavior. We therefore propose $\textbf{Co}$ntribution-$\textbf{Co}$ntrast ($\textbf{CoCo}$) response-level interpretation, which faithfully characterizes experts' roles using chosen-rejected response pairs with the largest contribution contrasts, jointly capturing routing and preference behavior. Across automatic and human evaluations, CoCo yields more coherent, faithful, and specialized interpretations than router-based, score-based, and sparse autoencoder-based alternatives while maintaining competitive reward modeling accuracy. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first systematic study of interpretation methods for MoE reward models.

</details>


### [80] [Interpretable Unsupervised Community Detection with LLM-Symbolized Structured Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06402)
*Aoting Zeng,Kai Wang,Jianwei Wang,Yuxiang Sun,Yizhang He,Wenjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LUCID是一种基于大语言模型引导的社区检测方法，无需训练和有标注数据，通过四阶段流程（初始化、合并、细化、选择）实现可解释且无监督的社区检测，在真实数据集上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于目标函数的社区检测方法难以应对复杂图结构，而深度学习方法虽然性能更好但缺乏可解释性且依赖标注数据和训练。LLM具有强大的推理能力和世界知识，有望实现可解释、无标注的社区检测，但目前尚未被充分利用。

Method: 受自然系统中相变动力学启发，LUCID设计为四阶段流程：(1) 局部视图社区初始化，通过k-ego上下文和无监督节点角色编码局部图结构；(2) 多因素社区合并，利用LLM诱导的规则迭代合并局部社区；(3) 多粒度社区细化，应用LLM诱导的由粗到细规则并行减少边界噪声；(4) 全局视图社区选择，基于拓扑紧凑性和边界清晰度识别高质量社区。LLM在流程中诱导形式化规则，将隐式知识转化为显式可解释的逻辑结构。

Result: 在真实数据集上的大量实验表明，LUCID作为无监督方法达到了最先进的性能，并持续优于领先的无监督和半监督基线方法。

Conclusion: LUCID成功地将LLM的推理能力与社区检测任务结合，实现了无需训练、无需标注且可解释的社区检测方法，验证了LLM在复杂图分析任务中的潜力。

Abstract: Community detection is a fundamental task in graph analytics that aims to identify cohesive groups of entities with similar behaviors or interests. Classic objective-driven methods struggle with complex graph structures, while deep-learning approaches improve performance at the expense of interpretability and rely on labeled data and training. Large language models (LLMs), with strong reasoning capabilities and world knowledge, are promising for interpretable, label-free community detection. To leverage these strengths, we propose LUCID, an LLM-guided, interpretable, training-free, and unsupervised community detection method. Inspired by phase-transition kinetics in natural systems, where complex structures emerge through initialization, merging, refinement, and selection, LUCID is designed as a four-stage pipeline. Within this pipeline, the LLM induces formal rules that translate implicit knowledge into explicit and interpretable logical structures. Specifically, (1) the Local-View Community Initialization stage encodes local graph structures using k-ego contexts and unsupervised node roles; (2) the Multi-factor Community Merge stage uses LLM-induced rules to iteratively merge local communities; (3) the Multi-grain Community Refinement stage applies LLM-induced coarse-to-fine rules in parallel to reduce boundary noise; and (4) the Global-view Community Selection stage identifies high-quality communities based on topological compactness and boundary clarity. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate that LUCID, as an unsupervised approach, achieves state-of-the-art performance and consistently outperforms leading unsupervised and semi-supervised baselines.

</details>


### [81] [ADIAS: Automated Design of Interactive Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06410)
*Lekang Jiang,Bohan Tang,Stephan Goetz,Yiwen Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"以问题为中心"（issue-centric）的自动化代理设计框架ADIAS，通过显式维护持久的"问题状态"来引导优化过程，替代现有以候选者为中心的范式，在五个交互式基准上平均超越最强基线25.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化代理设计方法以候选者为中心组织跨轮次经验，导致修复进度隐式化、修复目标定位效率低下、部分进展整合缓慢、无效干预跨轮次传播等问题。

Method: 将代理优化形式化为"以问题为中心的代理优化"，并实例化为ADIAS框架，包含两个核心机制：(1) 持久问题状态——维护稳定的问题身份、生命周期状态、支撑证据和干预-结果历史；(2) 问题引导优化——利用该状态联合提议修复目标和修订方向，进行聚焦的全代码修改。

Result: 在五个交互式基准上，ADIAS平均超越最强基线25.2%，在四个骨干模型上取得一致提升；消融实验表明移除持久问题状态或替换为候选者中心策略会导致性能下降高达40.7%。

Conclusion: 以问题为中心的显式状态维护能够显著提升自动化代理设计的修复效率和最终性能，验证了将修复进展作为显式持久状态引导优化的有效性。

Abstract: Automated agent design improves agent harnesses through iterative revision, evaluation, and feedback summarization. Existing methods are largely candidate-centric: cross-round experience is organized around candidate agents, which leaves the repair progress implicit. This causes inefficient repair targeting, slow consolidation of partial progress, and propagation of ineffective interventions across rounds. Therefore, we formulate issue-centric agent optimization, in which repair progress is carried forward as an explicit persistent issue state to guide optimization, rather than re-derived from candidate history in each round. We instantiate the formulation in ADIAS, a framework for automated full-code agent design with two mechanisms. A persistent issue state maintains stable issue identities, lifecycle status, supporting evidence, and intervention-outcome histories. Issue-guided optimization uses this state to jointly propose repair targets and revision directions for subsequent focused full-code modification. Across five interactive benchmarks, ADIAS outperforms the strongest baseline by 25.2% on average and achieves consistent gains across four backbone models. Controlled ablations further show that removing persistent issue state or replacing issue-centric revision with candidate-centric policies leads to performance drops of up to 40.7%.

</details>


### [82] [Learning to Predict Middle-Layer Attention in MLLMs for Visual Token Prunin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06411)
*Yuyao Sun,Tao Deng,Shuang Li,Deqing Wang,Hao Geng,Minjun Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MAP（Middle-layer Attention Prediction）方法，通过问题对比教师选择机制识别样本特定的注意力层，并将该层的注意力蒸馏到轻量级预测器中，从而在送入语言模型之前就能预测视觉token的重要性并进行剪枝，无需在推理时计算注意力图。在LLaVA-NeXT-7B上，MAP仅保留5.56%的视觉token即可维持97.5%的性能，实现3.09倍端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在处理大量视觉token时效率低下。虽然已有研究利用中间层的文本到视觉注意力来指导视觉token剪枝，但存在两个问题：一是固定中间层的注意力对问题的响应程度因样本而异，导致固定的层选择次优；二是获取中间层注意力需要先让大量视觉token通过多个语言模型层，此时已消耗大量计算资源。

Method: MAP包含两个核心组件：(1) Question Contrastive Teacher Selection：通过对比原始问题与参考问题下的注意力差异，为每个样本识别最合适的教师层；(2) 从选定的教师层蒸馏注意力到轻量级预测器，该预测器仅基于多模态输入特征即可估计视觉token重要性。推理时，MAP结合预测的重要性分数与多样性准则，在进入第一个语言模型层之前完成视觉token剪枝，无需任何注意力图计算。

Result: 在十个基准数据集上，使用LLaVA-NeXT-7B模型，MAP仅保留5.56%的视觉token就维持了未剪枝模型97.5%的性能，实现了3.09倍的端到端加速。

Conclusion: MAP通过样本自适应的教师层选择和注意力蒸馏，解决了固定层剪枝次优和早期计算浪费的问题，实现了无需推理期注意力计算的高效视觉token剪枝，且与现有推理加速技术兼容。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) achieve strong performance across diverse vision-language tasks, but their efficiency is limited by the cost of processing numerous visual tokens. Visual token pruning can reduce this cost, but requires accurate token importance estimates. Recent studies have demonstrated that text-to-vision attention from middle language model layers can effectively guide visual token pruning, typically using attention from a predefined middle layer to select the visual tokens to retain. Two problems therefore remain. First, our analysis shows that the layer whose attention is most responsive to the question varies substantially across samples, making a fixed layer suboptimal. Second, obtaining attention from the appropriate middle layer requires processing numerous visual tokens through several language model layers, by which point considerable computation has already been spent. To address both problems, we propose Middle-layer Attention Prediction (MAP), which uses Question Contrastive Teacher Selection to identify a sample-specific teacher layer by contrasting attention under the original and reference questions, and distills attention from the selected layer into a lightweight predictor that estimates visual token importance from multi-modal input features. During inference, MAP combines the predicted importance scores with a diversity criterion to prune visual tokens before the first language model layer. Thus, MAP requires no attention maps for pruning and remains compatible with existing inference acceleration techniques. Across ten benchmarks on LLaVA-NeXT-7B, MAP retains 97.5% of the unpruned model performance with only 5.56% of the visual tokens, yielding a 3.09x end-to-end speedup.

</details>


### [83] [WebGrader: Training LLMs for Web Development with Self-Evolving Programmatic Grader](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06474)
*Boshui Chen,Huiping Liu,Shaolei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出WebGrader，一种自进化的程序化评分器，用于解决大语言模型生成完整网站时强化学习训练中的奖励设计瓶颈。它从网站需求自动推导交互流程，将其表示为可执行的Flow Contract，并以执行结果作为RL奖励，在WebGen-Bench上达到52.01%的功能成功率，超越多个基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成完整网站的能力日益增强，但强化学习训练受限于奖励设计：手写浏览器脚本虽然可执行但编写成本高，VLM和GUI-agent评分器虽可扩展但可能在决定性状态出现前就给出判定，导致奖励信号不准确。

Method: WebGrader包含四个核心组件：(1) 从每个网站请求自动推导交互流并表示为可执行Flow Contract；(2) 在真实浏览器中实例化生成的项目，将目标动作锚定到源代码和实时DOM；(3) 沿同一浏览器轨迹收集视觉、DOM、响应和持久状态证据；(4) 利用残差驱动的离线循环发现可复用的验证器技能，在不相交的验证页面上筛选，并在策略训练前冻结提升的技能图。整体将测试规划、动作锚定、证据收集和语义判断明确分离。

Result: 在WebGen-Bench上，WebGrader训练的8B策略达到52.01%的功能成功率，比匹配的外观加脚本奖励高出7.88个百分点，并超越o4-mini和DeepSeek-v4-flash。在WG-core-250上，策略达到44.953的Full Score，超越Qwen3-Coder-480B。

Conclusion: 通过将测试规划、动作锚定、证据收集和语义判断分离，WebGrader仅在观察到所请求的状态转换后才发出Pass判定，有效解决了奖励设计中的瓶颈问题。自进化的验证器技能发现和筛选机制进一步提升了奖励信号的质量和可复用性。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly generate complete websites from natural-language descriptions, and reinforcement learning has become a central approach to closing their remaining functional gap. This training regime is bottlenecked by reward design. Hand-authored browser scripts are executable yet costly to write for open-ended requirements, while VLM and GUI-agent graders scale but may issue verdicts before observing the decisive state. We propose WebGrader, a self-evolving programmatic grader that autonomously derives the required interaction flows from each website request, represents each flow as an executable Flow Contract, and uses its execution outcome as an RL reward. WebGrader materializes the generated project in a live browser, grounds target actions against the source code and live DOM, and collects visual, DOM, response, and persistent-state evidence along the same browser trajectory. A residual-driven offline loop then discovers reusable verifier skills, screens them on disjoint validation pages, and freezes the promoted skill graph before policy training. By separating test planning, action grounding, evidence collection, and semantic judgment, WebGrader issues a Pass verdict only after observing the requested transition. On WebGen-Bench, WebGrader trains an 8B policy to a 52.01% functional success rate, outperforming a matched appearance-plus-script reward by 7.88 points and surpassing o4-mini and DeepSeek-v4-flash. On WG-core-250, the policy reaches a Full Score of 44.953 and surpasses Qwen3-Coder-480B.

</details>


### [84] [KNOWPLAN: Knowledge-Driven AI Agents for Smart Degree Pathway Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06530)
*Shuheng Cao,Weijia Zhang,Jiaqi Wu,Xiyun Hu,Yat Yang,Juqy Chen,Zhaoxiang Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KnowPlan将学位规划拆分为两个严格解耦的阶段：先通过CatalogBrowse从异构官方来源重建课程目录，再通过DegreeMap基于重建结果优化个性化修课路径。两阶段通过三个带溯源链接的JSON文档衔接，在100所大学的测试中达到96.2%目录召回率和100%硬可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学位规划面临两个相互耦合的问题：课程目录重建和个性化路径优化。传统方法将两者耦合会导致失败模式互相掩盖，因为驱动自身爬取的规划器永远学不到当前计划不需要的事实。需要强制解耦提取与优化两个阶段。

Method: KnowPlan采用两阶段架构：CatalogBrowse进行提取优先的探索，通过期望边际增益评分、确定性平台适配器和span约束的clause-to-AST解析，以闭包证书而非奖励阈值终止；DegreeMap仅消费提取阶段的JSON文档，编译为类型化需求超图，用CP-SAT进行字典序优化（硬可行性、完成视野、负载与风险、个性化效用、期权价值）。

Result: 在100所大学的广泛轨道和6所学校的密集轨道上，CatalogBrowse达到96.2%库存召回率和88.7%掩码源恢复，比穷举爬虫少47%源访问；DegreeMap保持100.0%硬可行性，个性化效用比最强基线高+0.066；全管道认证99.5%请求，与特权金标图表的效用差距仅0.015。

Conclusion: 通过强制执行提取与优化的解耦边界并测量两阶段间的接口质量而非假设其完美，KnowPlan证明了先在无用户画像条件下完整重建课程目录、再在该证明最优范围内优化个性化路径的方案，可以同时保证可行性、效用和可认证性，优于耦合方案。

Abstract: Planning a degree from official university sources requires solving two problems in order. The institution's curriculum must first be reconstructed from catalogs, departmental pages, JSON endpoints, and PDFs that share no schema, and only then can a student-specific path be optimized under prerequisite logic and overlapping requirement constraints. Coupling the two lets each failure mode hide the other, because a planner that drives its own crawling never learns facts its current plan does not need. We present KnowPlan, which enforces an extraction-first boundary and measures the interface between the stages rather than assuming it. CatalogBrowse explores with no access to any user profile. It scores legal actions by lower-confidence expected marginal gain over a finite set of atomic catalog obligations per unit of source access, parses deterministically through platform adapters with a span-constrained clause-to-AST model fallback, and terminates on a closure certificate over index, schema, provenance, and reference completeness instead of a reward threshold. Its output contract is three provenance-linked JSON documents. DegreeMap consumes only those documents. It compiles them into a typed requirement hypergraph and optimizes lexicographically with CP-SAT over hard feasibility, completion horizon, load and risk, personalized utility, and option value, so that each stage optimizes inside the previous stage's proven optimum and stays certifiable within the solver budget. Across a 100-university broad track and a six-school dense track, CatalogBrowse reaches 96.2% inventory recall and 88.7% masked-source recovery at 47% less source access than an exhaustive crawler, DegreeMap holds 100.0% hard feasibility while improving personalized utility by +0.066 over the strongest baseline, and the full pipeline certifies 99.5% of requests with a utility gap to the privileged gold graph of 0.015.

</details>


### [85] [TaskSense: Focusing on What Matters in World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06544)
*SM Mazharul Islam,Manfred Huber*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TaskSense，一个以任务为中心的世界模型框架，通过可微分的随机空间注意力机制和辅助逆动力学目标，在视觉编码前强制执行任务相关性，仅重建被关注的区域而非完整观测，从而在视觉干扰环境下显著提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统世界模型通过重建完整观测来学习潜状态，但任务相关内容通常只占观测的一小部分，背景杂波和干扰物会消耗宝贵的表征容量。这种重建目标与控制目标之间的不匹配会稀释控制相关特征的学习信号，在视觉干扰下严重降低下游性能。

Method: TaskSense框架包含三个关键组件：(1) 可微分的随机空间注意力机制，以前一潜状态为条件，在潜编码前强制执行任务相关性；(2) 辅助逆动力学目标，引导注意力聚焦于控制相关区域；(3) 仅重建被关注区域而非完整观测的机制，解码器以采样的注意力图为条件，确保在随机注意力下也能实现一致的建。

Result: 与DreamerV3基线相比，TaskSense在DeepMind Control套件上保持竞争性表现，同时在Distracting Control套件上持续超越DreamerV3，显著提升了视觉干扰下的鲁棒性。定性分析证实学习到的注意力能够一致地定位控制相关区域并抑制无关视觉内容。

Conclusion: TaskSense验证了"先关注再建模"的设计理念在视觉控制世界模型中的有效性，表明通过强制任务相关性和仅重建关注区域，可以有效缓解视觉重建与控制目标之间的冲突，为在复杂视觉环境下构建更鲁棒的具身智能控制器提供了新思路。

Abstract: World models for visual control typically learn compact latent states by reconstructing observations, implicitly encouraging representations to preserve information across the entire visual input. However, task-relevant content often occupies only a small fraction of the observation, while background clutter and distractors consume valuable representational capacity. This mismatch between visual reconstruction and control objectives biases latent representations to model task-irrelevant visual content, diluting learning signals for control-relevant features and severely degrading downstream performance under visual distractions. We introduce TaskSense, a task-centric world modeling framework that enforces task relevance before latent encoding through a differentiable stochastic spatial attention mechanism conditioned on the previous latent state. To steer attention toward control-relevant regions, we augment training with an auxiliary inverse-dynamics objective. Rather than reconstructing the full observation, the world model reconstructs only the attended regions, encouraging latent representations to preserve task-relevant information while discarding irrelevant visual content. The decoder is further conditioned on the sampled attention map, enabling consistent reconstruction despite stochastic attention. Compared with the DreamerV3 baseline, TaskSense maintains competitive performance on the DeepMind Control Suite while consistently outperforming DreamerV3 on the Distracting Control Suite, demonstrating substantially improved robustness to visual distractions. Qualitative analysis further confirms that the learned attention, guided by inverse-dynamics supervision, consistently localizes control-relevant regions while suppressing irrelevant visual content.

</details>


### [86] [Divergent Response Modes in Frontier Language Models Under Steering Pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06578)
*Ali Jalal-Kamali*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究对六个开发者旗下的六款前沿大模型进行了行为可操控性（steerability）的系统评估，使用300对配对样本和24480条模型互评判断，发现不同模型在显式操控压力下不仅响应的转移程度不同，其响应模式（mode）也各异，部分模式仅出现在个别模型中。此外，通过线性探针在Llama的残差流中解码行为（准确率0.87），并可通过注入该方向将行为从0%驱动至86%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿语言模型在训练数据、目标和安全管线上各不相同，但这些差异是否会在显式操控压力下产生可测量的行为差异，此前尚未被充分探索。理解这一问题对于模型行为可控性、安全评估和模型研究都具有重要意义。

Method: 选取六个开发者的六款前沿模型，构建300对配对基础/操控样本（分为价值观冲突、推理激发、推理抑制三类，外加40个验证项）。所有六个模型作为盲审同行裁判，依据固定行为rubric对所有响应进行分类，共产生24480条判断，采用留一法共识评分。另以Llama为开源模型，通过线性探针从残差流解码行为，并在生成过程中注入该方向向量进行干预扫描。所有结果在token预算补救和假设盲判断提示的对照实验中验证。

Result: 模型在操控下不仅行为转变的程度不同，响应模式类型也有差异，部分模式仅出现在一两个模型中。GPT-5拒绝披露推理过程但保留答案（99%对比其他模型0%）；Claude Opus 4.7和GPT-5以不同方式抵抗显式抑制指令。Llama行为的最大分裂可在其内部解释：线性探针在残差流上以0.87的留出准确率解码行为，注入该方向可将行为从0%提升至86%。所有发现均通过token预算补救和对照实验验证。

Conclusion: 不同前沿模型的显式可操控性存在本质差异，这些差异既体现在操控敏感度上也体现在行为模式多样性上，且部分行为特征可通过模型内部表征进行解释和干预控制，说明模型行为差异不仅源于训练管线，也可能源于架构和表征层面的系统差异。

Abstract: Frontier language models are trained using distinct data, objectives, and safety pipelines. Whether these differences produce measurably different behaviors under explicit steering pressure remains underexplored. This study evaluates behavioral steerability across six frontier models from six developers using 300 paired base and steered items over three categories: values-conflict, reasoning-elicitation, and reasoning-suppression (plus 40 validation items). All six models act as blind peer judges and classify every response based on fixed behavioral rubrics. The resulting 24,480 judgments are scored by leave-one-out consensus. We find that models differ not just in how much steering shifts their behavior but in what kind (mode) of response they give, and some response modes appear in only one or two of them. GPT-5 deflects requests to disclose its reasoning while leaving its answer intact (99% vs. 0% for all other models).
