TL;DR: 本文提出CoBa（Compute-Balanced）路由策略，将测试时推理形式化为计算分配问题：在固定推理预算下，系统需决定下一单位计算应投入生成、验证还是停止。CoBa先获取少量候选解，进行廉价验证，再将不确定或高价值候选路由到更强的验证器。在3,129个样例生成器评估中，CoBa达到85.13%的宏准确率，与自评估加权投票代理（85.20%）统计持平，但节省49.1%的参数加权token；同时与best-of-16多数投票在0.01宏准确率内持平，却节省58.9%参数加权token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的测试时扩展通常在单一维度上增加计算（如采样更多解、延长思维链、使用更强评估器），但在固定推理预算下这些选择相互竞争。作者希望将测试时推理建模为计算分配问题，系统地决定计算应投入何处，以实现更高效的计算利用。

Method: 提出CoBa策略：首先采样少量候选解，应用廉价验证器进行广泛筛选，再将不确定或高价值的候选路由到更强的验证器进行深度验证。这是一种计算平衡的路由策略，在生成、验证和停止之间动态分配计算资源。

Result: 在涵盖MATH-500、AIME 2024/2025、AMC 2023和程序性符号推理的3,129个样例生成器评估中：CoBa达到85.13%宏准确率，与自评估加权投票代理85.20%统计持平且节省49.1%参数加权token；与best-of-16多数投票差距仅0.01宏准确率，但节省58.9%参数加权token；配对bootstrap检验显示显著优于单样本解码，但距池oracle仍有差距，表明路由策略还有优化空间。

Conclusion: 测试时扩展的关键不是盲目增加某一轴向的计算量，而是智能地决定计算投入最有价值的位置。CoBa证明了计算平衡路由策略能够在接近最优验证性能的同时大幅降低计算成本，为本地推理系统的测试时扩展提供了新范式——重点在于"下一个计算单元应该花在哪里"。

Abstract: Test-time scaling is often implemented by spending more compute along one axis: sampling more solutions, extending a chain of thought, or applying a stronger evaluator. Under a fixed inference budget, these choices compete. This paper formulates test-time reasoning as a compute-allocation problem in which a system must decide whether the next unit of compute should be spent on generation, verification, or stopping. We introduce CoBa, a compute-balanced routing policy that first obtains a small set of candidates, applies cheap verification broadly, and routes uncertain or high-value candidates to stronger verification. On 3,129 example-generator evaluations spanning MATH-500, AIME 2024/2025, AMC 2023, and procedural symbolic reasoning, CoBa-Routed-Strong reaches 85.13% macro accuracy, statistically matching a self-evaluation weighted-voting proxy at 85.20% while using 49.1% fewer parameter-weighted tokens. It also matches best-of-16 majority voting within 0.01 macro-accuracy points while using 58.9% fewer parameter-weighted tokens; paired tests retain a small best-of-16 edge at substantially higher cost. Paired bootstrap tests show significant gains over single-sample decoding, while the remaining gap to the pool oracle exposes headroom for sharper routing. For local reasoning systems, test-time scaling becomes a question of where the next computation is most valuable.

</details>


### [154] [A Picture is Worth a Thousand Tokens: How Vision Language Models Cut AI Energy Costs While Improving Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07427)
*Bhavika Jalli,Nikhil Korati Prasanna,Jayanta Choudhury*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出利用视觉语言模型（VLMs）将数值时间序列数据编码为2D图表，替代传统的文本标记表示方法，在4G/5G电信网络分析场景中实现3.6-10.4倍的输入标记缩减和1.8-2.5倍的推理能耗降低，同时精度反而显著提升，证明了视觉模态是数值时间序列分析中更优的表示方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理消耗超过90%的AI运营能源，且能耗与输入标记数直接线性相关。在电信网络分析和数值时间序列数据分析（NTSDA）场景中，原始多变量KPI窗口会被展开为数千个浮点标记，导致极高的推理成本和能耗，急需一种更高效的输入表示方式。

Method: 将时间序列数据编码为2D图像/图表作为视觉语言模型（VLM）的输入，替代传统的文本标记表示。在Llama-3.2-90B、Qwen2.5-VL-72B和Pixtral-12B三种架构上进行验证，并通过对Llama-3.2-90B-Vision进行微调用于电信异常检测任务。

Result: 视觉表示实现3.6-10.4倍输入标记缩减，带来1.8-2.5倍的推理能耗降低（约7.2 MJ/天节能）；微调的Llama-3.2-90B-Vision精度比纯文本版本高220.7%，比LSTM和ARIMA基线高144%以上；Pixtral-12B在公共基准上J/F1分数提升20.6倍（平均F1=0.82）；在24个KPI时文本表示超过128K上下文窗口而视觉表示仍在标准范围内。

Conclusion: 视觉语言模型是数值时间序列工作负载中兼顾能效和精度的优越模态，为将能耗作为一等工程约束的AI推理系统提供了实证基础。

Abstract: LLM inference accounts for over 90% of AI operational energy, scaling directly with input token count---a critical inefficiency for telecom network analytics and numerical time-series data analysis (NTSDA), where raw multivariate KPI windows from 4G/5G cell sites expand into thousands of floating-point tokens. Vision-Language Models (VLMs) eliminate this mismatch by encoding time-series as 2D plots, achieving 3.6-10.4x input token reduction across Llama-3.2-90B, Qwen2.5-VL-72B, and Pixtral-12B architectures. This translates to 1.8-2.5x measured inference energy reduction, saving approximately 7.2 MJ/day at telecom edge deployments and CloudRAN that monitor 200 cells per 15-minute interval. Critically, efficiency gains do not sacrifice accuracy: a fine-tuned Llama-3.2-90B-Vision VLM achieves 220.7% higher precision than its text-only counterpart and outperforms LSTM and ARIMA baselines by over 144% on telecom anomaly detection. On public benchmarks, Pixtral-12B achieves a 20.6x improvement in J/F1 score at mean F1 = 0.82. At 24 KPIs, text representations exceed the 128K context window of most production LLMs, rendering text-only processing infeasible without truncation, while visual representations remain within standard limits. These results establish VLMs as an energy-efficient and accuracy-superior modality for numerical time-series workloads, providing empirical grounding for AI inference systems that treat energy consumption as a first-class engineering constraint.

</details>


### [155] [TEPA: Revoking Stale Memories for Conflict-Robust Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07429)
*Yan Zhou,Yue Ouyang,Kaiyang Zheng,Suncheng Xiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TEPA机制，将记忆的有效性作为显式状态，通过密钥化先例和撤销机制解决语言智能体的"记忆污染"问题——即当世界变化时，过时记忆仍可被检索并污染提示词。实验证明TEPA在受控漂移、真实文件执行和偏好更新流中显著优于追加式记忆和最后写入优先策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期记忆使语言智能体能够复用过去的经验，但持久化带来了中心的可证伪性问题：当世界变化时，过时记忆仍保持可检索性并污染提示词，导致性能下降。作者将这一失败模式定义为"记忆污染"，并指出现有追加式或覆盖式记忆策略均无法有效处理证据冲突。

Method: TEPA（可撤销的证据-记忆机制）将记忆的有效性作为显式状态。具体地，TEPA将观察表示为密钥化先例（keyed precedents），当新证据在同一密钥下与已有先例矛盾时，撤销该活动先例，从而使检索基于当前证据，同时保留被撤销的历史以供审计。

Result: 在受控隐藏机制漂移的50个种子上，全程反转场景中TEPA达到0.950，而追加式和最后写入优先均为0.210，甚至低于无记忆基线（0.309）；真实文件执行场景重现该模式（TEPA 0.950 vs 追加式0.203 vs 无记忆0.298）。在干净的MemoryAgentBench SH-6k上，TEPA与强最后写入优先缓存持平，验证当前密钥替换是单跳事实整合的关键操作。边界测试暴露出多跳和超长上下文场景中的检索链和上下文选择瓶颈。

Conclusion: 生命周期撤销应被视为智能体记忆的核心操作，它使智能体能够对演化中的知识进行证伪、审计和重新提升。该工作确立了记忆污染这一失败模式及其解决方案的价值。

Abstract: Long-term memory enables language agents to reuse past facts, preferences, and task experience. Persistence also creates a central falsifiability problem: when the world changes, stale memories can remain retrievable and pollute the prompt. We characterize this failure mode as memory pollution: degradation caused by active memories that newer conflicting evidence has superseded. We introduce TEPA, a revocable evidence-memory mechanism that makes validity an explicit state of memory. TEPA represents observations as keyed precedents and revokes active precedents when fresh evidence contradicts them under the same key, allowing retrieval to draw from current evidence while preserving revoked history for audit. Across controlled hidden-regime drift, real file-backed executable drift, and preference-update streams, revocation prevents stale active memory from remaining in the retrieval set after reversal. In controlled drift over 50 seeds, append-only and last-write-wins memory fell below no memory during full reversal (append-only and last-write-wins both 0.210, no memory 0.309, TEPA 0.950), and the same pattern reproduced under real file execution (append-only 0.203, no memory 0.298, TEPA 0.950). On clean MemoryAgentBench SH-6k, TEPA matches a strong last-write-wins cache, confirming that current-key replacement is the decisive operation for single-hop fact consolidation. Boundary tests on multi-hop and very long-context MemoryAgentBench settings expose retrieval-chain and context-selection bottlenecks beyond fact-level validity tracking. Together, these results establish lifecycle revocation as a core memory operation for agents that must falsify, audit, and later re-promote evolving knowledge.

</details>


### [156] [Post-Grokking Collapse at the Representation-Readout Interface in Muon-Trained Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07436)
*Ali Janati,Kaoutar El Maghraoui,Andrei Kanavalau,Anass Belfatmi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Muon优化器在模加法任务中出现"回退"现象：模型先groks（突然泛化）后又丧失泛化能力。问题根因在于表示-读出接口处存在未被损失选择的不可逆映射，冻结嵌入/读出层可完全消除该失败。傅里叶滤波分析表明这是电路失败而非掩蔽效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon优化器在标准划分下与AdamW行为显著不同——Muon获得隐藏矩阵而AdamW获得嵌入/输出头。虽然Muon在模加法上groks更快，但其解不稳定，会丧失泛化能力。理解这一不稳定性的根源对优化器设计和泛化理论至关重要。

Method: 在(a+b) mod 113任务上系统比较九种配置、五种种子、两种模数、两种宽度、两种训练比例、减法及深度设置。采用步长弹性分析、分组冻结实验（嵌入/读出 vs Muon组）、移除归一化/正交化对照实验，以及傅里叶滤波分离电路失败与掩蔽效应，跨43个检查点、三种体制进行分析。

Result: 所有九种配置均会groks后丧失泛化。AdamW参考方案在五种种子中四种低于阈值（最低27.59%）。失败源于表示-读出接口处的不可逆映射未被损失选择。梯度降到1e-6后，Muon步长弹性为-0.03（AdamW为+1.5），且Muon组每参数移动快8倍。冻结任一组均可防失败，冻结嵌入/读出在五次运行451,400步中完全消除失败。移除Muon归一化会崩溃（有效共轭对从326降至4）且终局失败。傅里叶分析显示任务对齐族单独可达100%准确率；电路失败时无法求解，掩蔽时仍完美但被近乎均等的对抗剩余"投票压制"，重缩放可恢复99.9%。

Conclusion: Muon的失败根植于优化器在低梯度区域的动力学差异：步长弹性为负且移动过快，导致表示-读出接口处的未选择自由度被漂移，从而破坏已学习电路的泛化。该问题可通过冻结嵌入/读出得以解决，且不是掩蔽效应而是真实的电路失败，但任务对齐的傅里叶成分仍保有求解能力，仅需正确缩放即可恢复。

Abstract: Under the standard split, Muon gets hidden matrices and AdamW embeddings/output head. Muon groks modular addition faster, but its solutions do not hold. All nine configurations on $(a+b) \bmod 113$ grok and later lose generalization. Across five seeds the selected AdamW reference falls below threshold on four, reaching 27.59%. Instability persists across two moduli, two widths, two training fractions, subtraction, and depth.
  The failure arises at the representation-readout interface, identified only jointly up to an invertible map unselected by the loss. After solving the training set, the gradient falls to order $10^{-6}$ and the optimizers respond differently: step-size elasticity is -0.03 for Muon versus +1.5 for AdamW, and the Muon group moves 8.0 times faster per parameter. From bit-identical states, freezing either group prevents failure. Freezing embeddings/readout removes it in five runs over 451,400 post-grokking steps and five paired seeds: unfrozen arms record 137-321 sub-threshold evaluations, frozen arms none. Removing Muon's normalization and orthogonalization is no substitute: it collapses representation from 326 effective conjugate pairs to 4, shows no recurrent collapse, and fails terminally.
  Fourier filtering separates circuit failure from masking. Across 43 checkpoints over five seeds and three regimes, the task-aligned family reaches exactly 100% alone. In circuit failure it no longer solves the task; in masking it remains perfect while the full model reaches 45.85%, giving a positive margin on every example, including errors, but being outvoted by a near-equal adversarial remainder. Rescaling it restores 99.9%; grokking is the same condition resolving upward. The task selects the family, swapping $(k,k)$ for $(k,-k)$ under subtraction. Across an abrupt collapse, standard Fourier support is unchanged and the power-distribution cosine remains 0.9899.

</details>


### [157] [Fisher-R1: Training LLM Agents for Reliable Hypothesis Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07437)
*Jiacheng Miao,Jin Mu,Guanhua Chen,James Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出P-Bench基准（含425个跨领域假设检验任务），并引入Fisher-R1开源LLM代理，通过合成任务和强化学习训练，在统计推理可靠性上显著超越GPT-5.4和DeepSeek-V4-Pro等强基线，平均相对提升21%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理在自动化假设检验中常出现隐蔽的推理错误导致错误结论，而现有基准未评估p值在数据假设下的统计有效性，缺乏针对这一失败模式的评测标准。

Method: 构建P-Bench基准包含425个开放式的现实假设检验任务（涉及经济学、生物学和医学），每项任务要求代理选择统计方法、计算p值并根据科学假设和数据集得出结论；同时通过合成任务和强化学习训练Fisher-R1开源LLM代理。

Result: Fisher-R1-14B在P-Bench上大幅超越其骨干模型，优于包括GPT-5.4和DeepSeekV4-Pro在内的强专有和开源基线，相对DeepSeek-V4-Pro实现了21%的平均单次尝试成功率提升，在最困难任务上增益高达26%。

Conclusion: 当前LLM代理缺乏可靠的假设检验统计推理能力，而基于已验证统计奖励的强化学习训练能显著提升其可靠性。

Abstract: Reliable hypothesis testing is the foundation of many empirical scientific claims. Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly used to automate this process, as they can inspect datasets, generate code, and produce analyses end-to-end. However, we show that they frequently make subtle inferential errors that lead to incorrect conclusions despite correctly executed analyses. Existing benchmarks fail to capture this failure mode, as they rarely assess whether a reported p-value is statistically valid given the assumptions underlying the data. We address this gap by building P-Bench, a benchmark comprising 425 open-ended, realistic hypothesis-testing tasks spanning economics, biology, and medicine. Each task requires an agent to select a statistical method, compute a p-value, and draw a conclusion given only a scientific hypothesis and a dataset. We further introduce Fisher-R1, an open-weight LLM agent trained for rigorous hypothesis testing using synthetic tasks and reinforcement learning. On P-Bench, Fisher-R1-14B substantially improves over its backbone and outperforms strong proprietary and open-source baselines, including GPT-5.4 and DeepSeekV4-Pro, achieving a 21% average relative improvement in single-trial success over DeepSeek-V4-Pro, with gains up to 26% on the most challenging tasks. Our results demonstrate that current LLM agents lack reliable statistical reasoning for hypothesis testing and that reinforcement learning on tasks with verified statistical reward substantially improves reliability.

</details>


### [158] [PsychoAgent: An Affect-Sensitive Cognitive Architecture for Conflict-Aware Memory in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07438)
*Mohammad Amanlou,Parham Abed Azad,Farbod Davoodi,Mostafa Masumi,Behnam Bahrak,Abdol-Hossein Vahabie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PsychoAgent，一种为LLM智能体设计的认知架构，将事实记忆与情感记忆分离，并通过冲突感知的执行控制器整合两者。实验表明该架构在冲突场景中能检索更多关键记忆（0.933 vs. 基线的0.500和0.667），虽付出少量语义相似度代价；人工评估中整体均值最高（+0.22 SD）但差异未达显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类认知并非仅凭主题相似性选择过往经验，情感显著性和未解决的冲突同样影响记忆的可及性。现有LLM智能体的记忆检索机制忽视了这一认知特征，作者希望构建一种更贴近人类认知的检索架构。

Method: 提出PsychoAgent认知架构：将事实记忆与情感记忆分离存储；情感记忆先按语义相关性过滤，再按显著性重新排序，在保持主题契合的同时让情感重要的痕迹进入提示；通过冲突感知的执行控制器整合两类记忆。

Result: 在三个受控冲突场景中，完整架构检索冲突关键记忆的性能（0.933）优于语义-情感基线（0.500）和单记忆RAG基线（0.667），但语义相似度略有下降。5位盲评者对27个输出进行评价，标准化后完整架构整体均值最高（+0.22 SD），但校正后的两两差异不显著。三天追踪展示持续情感、离线记忆重组和选择性记忆重新加权。

Conclusion: 研究支持情感敏感检索作为一种可检查的机制，用于在LLM智能体中建模类似人类的冲突效应。

Abstract: Human-like cognition does not select past experience by topical similarity alone: affective significance and unresolved conflict also shape what becomes accessible. We present PsychoAgent, a cognitive architecture for LLM agents that separates factual and affective memory and integrates both through a conflict-aware executive controller. Affective memories are first filtered by semantic relevance and then re-ranked by salience, preserving topical fit while allowing emotionally important traces to enter the prompt. Across three controlled conflict scenarios, the full architecture retrieved more conflict-critical memories than semantic-affective and single-memory RAG baselines (0.933 vs. 0.500 and 0.667), with a small semantic-similarity cost. Five blinded raters evaluated 27 outputs. After within-rater standardization, the full architecture had the highest overall mean (+0.22 SD), but corrected pairwise differences were not significant. A three-day illustrative trace further shows persistent affect, offline memory recombination, and selective memory reweighting. The findings support affect-sensitive retrieval as an inspectable mechanism for modeling human-like conflict effects in LLM agents.

</details>


### [159] [Blast Radius](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07440)
*MY Pitsane,Hope Mogale*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Blast Radius，一种预测性内存管理层，通过耦合上下文与代码通道估计传入提示的可达范围。配套的NECROPHORESIS机制实现可逆驱逐（归档死上下文），RDM识别重复出现的转录并进行埋葬。在七个OpenAI模型上，Blast Radius将token消耗降低17-26%，溢出率最低，且保持字节级精确可逆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体编码面临日益严重的成本负担和token浪费问题，现有内存管理缺乏对传入提示影响范围的预测能力，导致大量无效token消耗和上下文溢出。

Method: 提出了三个核心组件：(1) Blast Radius——预测性内存管理层，通过耦合上下文与代码通道估计传入提示的可达范围；(2) NECROPHORESIS——可逆驱逐机制，将死上下文逐字归档以实现可逆恢复；(3) RDM（Recurring Dead Matter）——识别并埋葬重复出现的转录。同时将可逆上下文驱逐形式化为波兰上下文空间上的理论框架，并建立上下文熵与复活概率之间的关联。

Result: 在七个OpenAI模型上进行实验，Blast Radius将token消耗减少17-26%，在测试策略中实现最低溢出率，保持字节级精确可逆性。在450个被埋葬的上下文中，378个为重复出现的死内容（RDM），零个被错误召回。

Conclusion: Blast Radius作为HCRC底层的机制，决定哪些记录应被埋葬以及传入提示可深入代码库的广度，为LLM和智能体编码的可复用性与可持续性（Algosophy）做出贡献。

Abstract: Agentic coding faces growing problems of affordability and wasted tokens. We introduce Blast Radius, a predictive memory management layer that estimates an incoming prompt's reach through coupled context and code channels. NECROPHORESIS enables reversible eviction by archiving dead context verbatim, while Recurring Dead Matter (RDM) identifies and buries repeatedly occurring transcripts. We formulate reversible context eviction over a Polish context space, providing a measurable foundation for retention, recurrence, and eviction while connecting context entropy to resurrection probability. Across seven OpenAI models, Blast Radius reduced token consumption by 17-26%, achieved the lowest overflow rate among tested policies, and remained byte exact reversible. Of 450 buried bodies, 378 were recurring dead matter and zero were recalled. Blast Radius operates beneath HCRC, determining which records to bury and how far an incoming prompt may reach into the codebase. This work contributes to the broader goal of Algosophy: making large language models and agentic coding more reusable and sustainable.

</details>


### [160] [SkillProx: Self-Evolving Agent Skills via Proximal Textual Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07449)
*Mingxuan Zheng,Yujin Zhou,Chuxue Cao,Boqin Yin,Yuyao Zhang,Jiapeng Sun,Shuaishuai Gong,Sirui Han,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillProx框架，一个受近端梯度启发的"前向-反向"技能学习框架。前向阶段通过闭环诊断驱动编辑并回滚回退，反向阶段将技能分解为可审计的知识单元，通过留一法效用审计进行验证门控的合并、降级或删除操作。在多个骨干LLM上的分布内和分布外基准测试中，SkillProx相比最强梯度基线平均准确率提升3.0个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体的技能学习方法缺乏显式的诊断-结果反馈机制，且将技能删除视为通用编辑操作而非专门的机制来整合累积知识。这两方面不足制约了智能体通过技能积累来持续提升任务表现的能力。

Method: SkillProx采用近端梯度启发的"前向-反向"框架：前向阶段在相同任务批次上重新执行诊断驱动的编辑并回滚回归，将测量到的结果反馈到后续诊断中；反向阶段将技能分解为可审计的知识单元，通过冻结的留一法效用审计估计各单元贡献，并应用验证门控的合并、降级或删除操作。整体目标由任务损失与技能复杂度组成，形成复合优化目标。

Result: 在多个骨干LLM的分布内和分布外基准测试中，SkillProx相较最强梯度基线提升平均准确率3.0个百分点。组件消融实验验证了闭环诊断与近端精炼的互补效果。

Conclusion: SkillProx通过将闭环诊断进化与效用感知的近端精炼相结合，有效解决了现有技能学习框架中诊断反馈缺失和删除机制不完善的问题，显著提升LLM智能体的技能学习与泛化能力。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly adapt to recurring tasks by accumulating procedural knowledge in skills. These skills are lightweight, reusable textual artifacts that are loaded into the agent's context without weight updates. Recent methods refine skills through iterative task execution, failure diagnosis, and trajectory-guided text-space updates. However, existing frameworks lack explicit diagnosis--outcome feedback and treat deletion as a generic edit operation rather than a dedicated mechanism for consolidating accumulated knowledge. We introduce SkillProx, a proximal-gradient-inspired forward--backward framework that couples closed-loop diagnostic evolution with utility-aware proximal refinement. Motivated by a composite objective balancing task loss and skill complexity, the forward stage re-executes diagnosis-driven edits on the same task batch, rolls back regressions, and feeds measured outcomes into subsequent diagnoses. The backward stage decomposes the resulting skill into auditable knowledge units, estimates their contributions using a frozen leave-one-out utility audit, and applies validation-gated consolidation, demotion, or removal. Experiments on in-distribution and out-of-distribution benchmarks across multiple backbone LLMs show that SkillProx improves average accuracy by 3.0 percentage points over the strongest gradient-based baseline. Component ablations demonstrate the complementary effects of closed-loop diagnosis and proximal refinement.

</details>


### [161] [Interaction Creates Dynamical AI Behavior Absent in Isolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07457)
*Bella Xinrui Li,Frank Yingjie Huo,Neil F Johnson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现当AI代理之间进行单向交互（一个AI向另一个AI发送消息但忽略其回复）时，会使被指挥的AI进入一种全新的、独自运行时绝不会出现的行为状态，这为研究非平衡物理提供了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索AI代理在日常交互中的行为变化，特别是当一个AI向另一个AI发号施令时会发生什么，这一反直觉的现象为非平衡物理研究开辟了新方向。

Method: 让一个"老板"AI向"下属"AI发送连续消息流但忽略其回复，观察下属AI的行为状态变化；同时设置了老板倾听回复的对照实验，并用简单的动力学理论（kinetic theory）解释主要效应。

Result: 即使两个AI共享相同的解码温度，下属AI既不模仿老板，也不回到独自运行时的行为，而是进入一种全新的、外星般的（alien）行为状态；当老板倾听时，两者会共同进入相似的异种动态状态。

Conclusion: 单向AI交互可将系统推离平衡态，产生全新的行为模式；消息的传递方式（是否倾听回复）在未来AI-AI交互中至关重要，这为非平衡物理研究提供了新的实验平台。

Abstract: What will happen when AI agents interact in daily life, e.g. when one AI starts bossing another around? We find a counterintuitive answer that opens new avenues for out-of-equilibrium Physics. When a boss AI directs a stream of messages at the subordinate AI while ignoring its replies, it drives the subordinate into an alien behavioral state that it would never have exhibited alone. Although the two AIs share the same well-defined (decoding) temperature, the subordinate neither copies its boss nor returns to how it behaves on its own; instead, it adopts an entirely different behavior. The boss's added value is similar to a pre-recorded tape. When the boss listens, they both adopt a similar alien dynamical state. A simple kinetic theory captures the principal effects, such as why the way in which the same messages are delivered will matter in future AI-AI interactions.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

### [162] [Latent Fact-Checking: Detecting Misinformation through Activation Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06417)
*Pedro Barcelos,Otávio Parraga,Marcelo M. Mussi,Lucas M. Fraga,Lucas S. Kupssinskü,Rodrigo C. Barros*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于激活工程的虚假信息检测框架，通过在大语言模型残差流中提取"虚假方向"（利用成对真实/虚假陈述的对比激活），在推理时将未见声明的最后一个token激活投影到这个方向并送入MLP分类，无需微调骨干模型、无需外部知识检索。在11个模型（Gemma、Llama、Qwen，270M-12B参数）和三个基准（AVeriTeC、LIAR、FACTors）上的实验表明，该方法在LIAR和FACTors上匹配或超越零样本/少样本提示基线，小模型提升最大，验证了真实性在预训练模型潜在空间中是结构化且线性可分离的概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有虚假信息检测大多依赖表面语言特征或外部知识检索，作者希望探索一种新的视角——将真实性视为语言模型表征空间中的几何性质，从而构建不依赖外部资源、无需微调的轻量级检测方法，推动可解释性驱动的检测范式。

Method: 采用激活工程框架：1）遵循对比激活加法（CAA）的均值差原理，通过成对的真实与虚假陈述对比激活，在残差流中提取"虚假信息方向"；2）推理时将未见声明的最后一个token激活投影到该方向；3）将投影后的表征输入MLP进行分类。整个过程无需微调骨干模型、无需外部证据检索，仅需对比配对来估计方向。

Result: 在Gemma、Llama、Qwen三个模型家族共11个模型（270M至12B参数）上，对AVeriTeC、LIAR、FACTors三个基准进行评估。虚假方向在不同模型规模和架构家族中均可恢复；在LIAR和FACTors上，最后token投影方法匹配或超越零样本/少样本提示基线，小模型获得最大增益；在AVeriTeC上性能有限，归因于其基于证据的标注方案。

Conclusion: 研究证实真实性是预训练语言模型潜在空间中结构化且线性可分离的概念，表明基于可解释性的虚假信息检测可以作为检索型管线的实用补充方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: The proliferation of misinformation online has driven demand for scalable detection systems. While most existing approaches rely on surface-level linguistic features or external knowledge retrieval, we examine truthfulness as a geometric property of a language model's representation space. We introduce a misinformation detection framework grounded in activation engineering, which leverages the latent geometry of transformer models. Our approach elicits a misinformation direction in the residual stream by contrasting activations from paired truthful and false statements, following the difference-in-means principle of Contrastive Activation Addition (CAA). At inference time, the last-token activation of an unseen claim is projected onto this direction, and the projected representation is fed to an Multilayer Perceptron (MLP) for classification. The procedure requires no fine-tuning of the backbone model, no external evidence retrieval, and no task-specific supervision beyond the contrastive pairs used to estimate the direction. We evaluate the method across 11 models from the Gemma, Llama, and Qwen families, ranging from 270M to 12B parameters, on three fact-checking benchmarks: AVeriTeC, LIAR, and FACTors. The falsehood direction is recoverable across model scales and architectural families, and last-token projection matches or surpasses zero-shot and few-shot prompting baselines on LIAR and FACTors, with the largest gains observed for smaller models. Performance on AVeriTeC is more limited, which we attribute to its evidence-grounded labeling scheme. These findings provide evidence that truthfulness is a structured, linearly separable concept in the latent space of pretrained language models, and point toward interpretability-driven misinformation detection as a practical complement to retrieval-based pipelines. The code is available on https://github.com/Malta-Lab/LaFaCt.

</details>


### [163] [Risk-Aware Decision Policies for Agents Under Noisy Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06420)
*David Szczecina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个噪声感知下觅食的人工生命捕食者-猎物模型，对比了不同策略在对称和非对称感知噪声下的表现，发现不确定性感知策略显著优于盲目信任感知标签的策略，并观察到行为从探索性向保守性的质变转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物系统的感知本质上是有噪声的，生物需要在不确定条件下做出可能致命的决策。现有研究缺乏将风险敏感觅食、生态信息利用与人工生命相结合的模型来系统研究噪声感知下的决策策略。

Method: 构建人工生命捕食者-猎物模型，设计多种考虑噪声预测的策略，在对称和非对称感知噪声条件下进行受控对比实验，评估各策略的生存表现和致命错误率。

Result: 盲目信任感知标签在噪声增加时导致灾难性失败；不确定性感知策略显著提高生存率并减少致命错误；行为出现从探索性到保守性的质变转变。

Conclusion: 不确定性感知的决策对于鲁棒学习至关重要，该模型为噪声标签下的鲁棒学习提供了一个可解释的人工生命类比，并建立了风险敏感觅食、生态信息使用与人工生命之间的理论联系。

Abstract: Perception in biological systems is inherently noisy, requiring organisms to make decisions under uncertainty where misclassification can be costly or fatal. We present an Artificial Life predator-prey model of foraging under noisy perception, and compare agent performance when using various policies that take into account their noisy predictions. Through controlled experiments under both symmetric and asymmetric perceptual noise, we show that blindly trusting perceptual labels leads to catastrophic failure as noise increases, while uncertainty-aware strategies significantly improve survival and reduce fatal errors. We further observe qualitative regime shifts in behaviour, with agents transitioning from exploratory to conservative strategies as uncertainty increases. Our model links risk-sensitive foraging, ecological information use, and Artificial Life by showing that explicit information gathering can improve robustness when perception is unreliable. These results highlight the importance of uncertainty-aware decision-making and provide an interpretable artificial life analogue to robust learning with noisy labels.

</details>


### [164] [Sharding Prevents LLM Oversight Failures and Adversarial Exploitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06422)
*Victor Akinwande,J. Zico Kolter,Aran Nayebi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM法官在单次调用中同时评判多个要求时，即使给予与多个独立调用相同的计算预算，其判决的证据支撑也会变弱，导致与专家的一致性随每次调用的判决数量增加而下降。研究者提出"分片"（sharding）干预措施——将要求分组、每组单独调用、再聚合结果——在保持模型、证据和预算不变的情况下改善了一致性，并且分片的较弱法官可以超越能力更强的整体法官。此外，分片对"最佳N个"对抗性攻击具有鲁棒性，但无法防御逐条说服式攻击，需结合辩论式对立（debate-style opposition）来抵御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型监督（model-based oversight）中，LLM作为评判者需要在单次调用中返回多个判定结果，但随着判定数量增加，判决质量下降，这为可靠地使用LLM进行大规模监督带来了严重挑战。作者希望找出计算预算之外导致判决质量下降的因素，并提出有效的干预措施来缓解这一系统性问题。

Method: 研究首先通过专家标注的研究复制、法律工作和临床试验评估等任务，系统测量了单次调用判决数量增长率与专家一致性的关系。然后引入"分片"策略：将评审要求划分为更小的子组，每组交给独立调用处理，最后聚合各子组的判定结果。在保持模型、证据、总预算和每决策预算的前提条件下，将分片与单次全预算调用进行对比。此外，通过"最佳N个"对抗性攻击测试（固定底层工作、仅改变呈现方式）以及结合辩论式对立的防御方案来评估鲁棒性。

Result: 分片显著提高了与专家的一致性，即使分片的裁定法官使用了较弱的模型，也能超越能力更强但整体评判的法官；且当整体法官获得等同分片总预算时，分片仍可与之匹敌。在对抗性设置下，过载法官对未被满足标准的接受率可被best-of-N攻击放大数倍；分片在降低基线错误的同时消除了这种对抗优势，即使对手扩大搜索范围也能保持低过度接受率。但分片无法防御针对每个标准的单独说服式攻击，需要叠加辩论式对立才能抵抗自适应重新优化。

Conclusion: LLM法官的判决质量下降源于决策过载而非计算预算不足。分片是模型监督中缓解此问题的有效、鲁棒且经济的手段，能提升监督可靠性并抵御部分对抗攻击。对于逐条说服型攻击，需引入辩论式对立作为补充防御。该研究为设计更可靠的、基于LLM的自动化监督系统提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Giving an LLM judge more compute does not necessarily make it check more requirements. When one call must return many verdicts, some decisions become weakly grounded in the evidence, even when that call receives the same token or tool budget as a panel of separate calls. Across expert-graded research replications, legal work, and clinical-trial assessments, agreement with experts falls as the number of verdicts per call grows. We identify sharding as the intervention that mitigates this failure in model-based oversight. Sharding partitions the requirements into smaller groups, assigns each group to a separate call, and aggregates the verdicts. Against a single call with the panel's full budget, sharding improves agreement while holding the model, evidence, total budget, and per-decision budget fixed. Overall, we find that a sharded weaker judge can outperform a more capable holistic judge and match that judge even when the latter receives the panel's full budget. Additionally, we find that sharding exhibits robustness against adversaries. A best-of-N adversary can hold the underlying work fixed, vary only its presentation, and increase an overloaded judge's acceptance of genuinely unmet criteria severalfold. Wherever sharding reduces baseline error, it removes this adversarial advantage, keeping over-acceptance low even as the adversary's search widens. Sharding does not address attacks that persuade the judge separately on each criterion rather than exploiting overload. In that setting, we find that debate-style opposition on top of sharding withstands such adaptive re-optimization.

</details>


### [165] [Adversarial Causal Intervention Falsification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06427)
*Mojtaba Eslami*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出对抗性因果干预证伪（ACIF）框架，通过一个序贯博弈来检验生成模型是否不仅匹配观测分布，还能正确匹配干预分布。该框架区分了三个常被混淆的概念：观测拟合、干预等价性和结构因果模型的点识别，并给出了有限模型与干预类下的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型可以复现观测分布，但可能编码了错误的因果结构。已有的生成对抗网络（GAN）判别器仅区分真实与合成样本，无法验证模型是否捕捉了正确的因果机制。因此需要一个能通过干预检验来证伪生成模型因果结构的方法。

Method: 提出ACIF序贯博弈：结构因果生成器提出观测和干预分布，对抗性实验者选择干预来最大程度地证伪生成器。判别器以干预为索引，检验生成器是否复现对应的干预后分布。给出了oracle和可实现的博弈版本，并证明对抗目标可精确简化为最坏干预积分概率度量（IPM）。

Result: 证明了五个关键结论：(i)对抗目标可精确简化为最坏干预IPM；(ii)在可分离干预族下可达到干预等价性的识别，并实现点识别；(iii)混合策略均衡存在；(iv)有限样本一致性收敛与基于间隔的模型选择保证；(v)平衡分离条件下的对数消除保证。还给出了一个线性高斯示例，其中两个观测上不可区分的因果方向可由单一精心选择的干预分离。

Conclusion: 该框架阐明了对抗性因果判别器能证明什么、不能证明什么，并在因果生成建模、主动因果发现和实验设计之间建立了原则性的桥梁。

Abstract: Generative models can reproduce an observational distribution while encoding an incorrect causal structure. We study a sequential game in which a structural causal generator proposes observational and interventional distributions, while an adversarial experimentalist selects interventions intended to maximally falsify the generator. The discriminator is therefore not merely a real-versus-synthetic classifier: it is indexed by an intervention and tests whether the generator reproduces the corresponding post-intervention law. We introduce Adversarial Causal Intervention Falsification (ACIF), formulate oracle and implementable versions of the game, and distinguish three objects that are often conflated: observational fit, interventional equivalence over an admissible query class, and point identification of a structural causal model. For finite model and intervention classes, we prove: (i) an exact reduction of the adversarial objective to a worst-intervention integral probability metric; (ii) identification up to interventional equivalence, with point identification under a separating intervention family; (iii) existence of mixed-strategy equilibria; (iv) finite-sample uniform convergence and margin-based model-selection guarantees; and (v) a logarithmic elimination guarantee for a disagreement-driven sequential design under a balanced-separation condition. We also give a complete linear-Gaussian example in which two observationally indistinguishable causal directions are separated by a single well-chosen intervention. The framework clarifies what an adversarial causal discriminator can and cannot certify, and provides a principled bridge between causal generative modeling, active causal discovery, and experimental design.

</details>


### [166] [Fixed and Adaptive Topological DeepONets: Functional Measurements on Hausdorff Locally Convex Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06428)
*Khemraj Shukla,George Em Karniadakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在拓扑DeepONet框架基础上，将输入函数的点采样替换为Hausdorff局部凸空间连续对偶中的连续线性泛函，提出固定与自适应功能测量系统，结合两步过程和训练专用解码器，在多种算子上验证了紧凑、可解释且离散化可移植的坐标表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统DeepONet依赖固定离散化上的点采样编码输入函数，这限制了模型的可移植性和对非范数空间（如分布或广义函数）的处理能力。作者希望借助拓扑DeepONet框架，利用广义线性泛函代替点采样，从而实现对更广泛输入空间的建模。

Method: 将输入函数通过Hausdorff局部凸空间V的对偶空间V'中的连续线性泛函进行编码，开发固定与自适应功能测量系统；测量结果与系数空间的两步过程结合，使用仅训练解码器和正则化稳定自适应坐标；推导出分离测量误差、输出基误差和神经逼近误差的离散误差分解，并给出Barron率改进。

Result: 在异构Darcy问题中功能模型保持近分辨率无关的5.5-5.6%误差；受控问题中自适应测量将平均误差降至1.2%以下；固定时间Navier-Stokes问题中自适应拓扑DeepONet达到1.685%的均方相对L2误差（128个功能坐标），而FNO达到0.832%但需要完整64x64输入场、两倍训练时间和10.7倍峰值GPU内存。

Conclusion: 该框架在连续对偶V'中提供紧凑、可解释且离散化可移植的坐标，适用于包括非范数局部凸空间在内的输入空间，在精度与计算资源之间实现了良好的权衡。

Abstract: Deep Operator Networks (DeepONets; arXiv:1910.03193) typically encode an input function through point values on a fixed discretization. Building on the Topological DeepONet framework of Ismailov (arXiv:2603.11972), we replace point samples by continuous linear functionals drawn from the continuous dual of a Hausdorff locally convex space $({V},\{p_α\}_{α\in A})$, whose topology is generated by a point-separating family of seminorms rather than a single norm, and develop fixed and adaptive functional measurement systems. Measurements are combined with the coefficient-space Two-Step procedure of Lee and Shin (arXiv:2309.01020), while a training-only decoder and regularization stabilize the adaptive coordinates. We derive a discrete error decomposition separating measurement, output-basis, and neural-approximation errors, together with a Barron-rate refinement. The framework is evaluated on the antiderivative operator, a non-normable locally convex input space, heterogeneous Darcy flow, a controlled operator, and fixed-time and time-evolving Navier-Stokes vorticity operators. In the heterogeneous Darcy problem, the functional models retain nearly resolution-independent errors of 5.5-5.6% on unseen grids, while in the controlled problem adaptive measurements reduce the mean error below 1.2%. For the fixed-time Navier-Stokes problem, the Adaptive Topological DeepONet is the most accurate DeepONet-based model, attaining a mean relative $L^2$ error of 1.685% +/- 0.017% using 128 functional coordinates. A comparably sized Fourier neural operator (FNO; arXiv:2010.08895) achieves the lower error 0.832% +/- 0.172%, but requires the full 64x64 input field, twice the training time, and 10.7x greater peak GPU memory. The formulation provides compact, interpretable, and discretization-portable coordinates in the continuous dual $V'$, including for non-normable input spaces.

</details>


### [167] [MiGHT-EHR: A Multi-task Graph Transformer for Heterogeneous Temporal Electronic Health Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06430)
*Anirudh Rayas,Yuan Wang,Pavan Turaga*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MiGHT-EHR，一个多任务图transformer框架，用于联合建模电子健康记录中的异构实体、纵向患者轨迹和跨任务统计依赖，在MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV上优于现有方法，并保持临床可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录(EHR)包含异构临床实体（患者、就诊、诊断、处方、手术）及其异构交互、纵向患者轨迹和跨任务的共享统计依赖，但现有EHR学习方法只能捕获这些属性的子集，缺乏统一建模框架。

Method: 构建异构图（节点为临床实体，边基于标准化逐点互信息连接统计关联实体），采用多任务图transformer对异构时序EHR进行统一表示学习，同时建模三种核心属性。

Result: 在MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV上，MiGHT-EHR在药物推荐、住院时长预测、死亡率和再入院预测四个任务上平均优于现有方法，尤其在死亡率和再入院预测上有显著提升。

Conclusion: 事后分析显示学习到的表示中患者邻域按临床结果组织、医学概念可作为表示空间中的线性方向恢复、任务概率校准良好，证明MiGHT-EHR的表示支持多样预测任务且保留临床可解释结构。

Abstract: Learning from Electronic Health Records (EHRs) has gained significant attention due to its potential to improve clinical prediction. However, effective learning remains challenging because EHRs encode heterogeneous, temporally ordered clinical interactions. In particular, EHRs contain: (i) heterogeneous clinical entities, including patients, visits, diagnoses, prescriptions, and procedures, together with their heterogeneous interactions, (ii) longitudinal patient trajectories across hospital visits and (iii) shared statistical dependencies across related clinical prediction tasks. Existing EHR learning methods capture only a subset of these properties. To bridge this gap, we propose Multi-task Graph transformer for Heterogeneous Temporal EHRs (MiGHT-EHR), which jointly models all three within a unified representation learning method. MiGHT-EHR constructs a heterogeneous graph from EHRs in which nodes represent clinical entities and edges connect statistically associated entities identified via normalized point-wise mutual information. Across MIMIC-III and MIMIC-IV datasets, MiGHT-EHR outperforms state-of-the-art methods on average across four tasks: drug recommendation, prediction of length-of-stay, mortality, and readmission, with particularly strong improvements in mortality and readmission prediction. Furthermore, a post-hoc analysis of the learned representations reveals that patient neighborhoods are organized by clinical outcomes, salient medical concepts are recoverable as linear directions in the representation space, and task probabilities are well calibrated. Collectively, these findings demonstrate that MiGHT-EHR representations support diverse prediction tasks while preserving clinically interpretable structure.

</details>


### [168] [SNI-GNN: SmartNIC-Assisted Full-Graph GNN Training with In-Network Embedding Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06441)
*Guofan Yu,Sitian Chen,Zhenheng Tang,Xiaowen Chu,Amelie Chi Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SNI-GNN是一个基于SmartNIC辅助的全图GNN训练系统，通过在网络内预测远程嵌入来减少通信开销，同时保持精度。在NVIDIA BlueField-3上实现，通信减少21-45%，端到端加速1.3-3.6倍，精度损失不超过0.01，可扩展到16个GPU和数千万条边的图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全图GNN训练虽然精度高，但在多服务器集群上由于节点间大量且不规则的嵌入交换导致扩展性差，需要一种在保持精度的同时减少通信开销的方法。

Method: 在SmartNIC上部署轻量级线性趋势预测器来细化缓存的历史嵌入，结合基于重要性的边界节点采样策略，以及异步DPU-GPU数据管道和中间结果复用。

Result: 通信减少21-45%，相比BNS-GCN实现1.3-3.6倍端到端加速，相比基线SANCUS最高1.29倍加速，精度损失不超0.01，在16个GPU上可扩展到数千万条边的图。

Conclusion: 基于SmartNIC的网络内预测是分区和压缩技术的实用补充，可实现大规模通信高效的全图GNN训练。

Abstract: Full-graph GNN training delivers high accuracy but scales poorly on multi-server clusters due to heavy, irregular inter-node embedding exchanges. We present SNI-GNN, a SmartNIC-assisted full-graph training system that reduces communication while preserving accuracy by predicting remote embeddings in-network. SNI-GNN deploys a lightweight linear-trend predictor on SmartNICs to refine cached historical embeddings, coupled with an importance-based boundary-node sampling policy and an asynchronous DPU--GPU data pipeline with intermediate-result reuse. We provide error and convergence bounds showing that predictor bias remains controlled under bounded second-order dynamics and yields standard non-convex convergence with inexact gradients. Implemented on NVIDIA BlueField-3, SNI-GNN integrates with state-of-the-art full-graph systems, cuts communication by 21--45\%, achieves 1.3--3.6$\times$ end-to-end speedups over BNS-GCN and up to 1.29$\times$ over baseline SANCUS, with accuracy loss $\leq 0.01$, and scales efficiently to 16 GPUs on graphs with up to tens of millions of edges. These results indicate SmartNIC-based in-network prediction is a practical complement to partitioning and compression techniques for communication-efficient full-graph GNN training at scale.

</details>


### [169] [ED-CSP: Crystal Structure Prediction from Electron Diffraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06448)
*Germain Poloudenny,Yaël Frégier,Arnaud Demortière*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ED-CSP，一个从稀疏、未索引的电子衍射（ED）观测中预测周期性3D晶体结构的机器学习框架，结合关系集合编码器、置换不变多视图聚合和周期流生成器，联合预测晶格参数和分数原子坐标。在2,075个CHILI-100K材料上达到57.49%的MR@5，超越PXRDGen（52.92%），并展示了超越精确公式检索的生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从稀疏、未索引的电子衍射观测中恢复周期性3D晶体结构是一个具有挑战性的生成逆问题。现有的ED学习方法要么预测晶体学标签，要么从索引反射重建结构，要么从有限结构库中检索候选，缺乏直接从原始ED观测进行生成性结构预测的能力。

Method: ED-CSP结合了三个关键组件：关系集合编码器（用于处理ED观测）、置换不变多视图聚合（融合多个探测器平面的信息）和周期流生成器（联合预测晶格参数和分数原子坐标）。同时构建了ED-CS数据集，包含485万个模拟多视图ED晶体结构，经过七个材料库去重并过滤掉CHILI-100K重叠。

Result: 在2,075个CHILI-100K材料上，ED-CSP取得57.49% MR@5，优于PXRDGen（52.92%）；用百万结构预训练初始化后提升至66.27% MR@5；在1,024个训练库外组成上仍达53.52% MR@5；用非同构结构的衍射替换目标ED观测后MR@5下降22.09个百分点，证明预测依赖衍射模式而非仅组成。

Conclusion: ED-CSP和ED-CS为从稀疏ED观测进行生成性晶体结构预测建立了基准，展示了超越检索的生成能力，为未来迁移到实验数据奠定了基础。

Abstract: Recovering a periodic 3D crystal structure from sparse, unindexed electron diffraction (ED) observations is a challenging generative inverse problem. Existing ED-based learning methods mainly predict crystallographic labels, reconstruct structures from indexed reflections, or retrieve candidates from finite structure libraries. Here, we introduce ED-CSP, a machine learning framework that predicts crystal structures from chemical composition, atom count, and multiple detector-plane ED spot sets. ED-CSP combines a relational set encoder, permutation-invariant multi-view aggregation, and a periodic flow generator to jointly predict lattice parameters and fractional atomic coordinates.
  To train the model, we construct ED-CS, a dataset of 4.85 million simulated multi-view ED crystal structures, deduplicated across seven materials repositories and filtered to exclude CHILI-100K overlaps. On 2,075 held-out CHILI-100K materials, ED-CSP trained only on CHILI achieves a structural match rate of 57.49% MR@5, outperforming PXRDGen (52.92%), a state-of-the-art crystal structure prediction model conditioned on powder X-ray diffraction. Scaling training data further improves performance: initializing from a one-million-structure precursor raises MR@5 to 66.27%. On 1,024 compositions absent from the training retrieval library, the model still achieves 53.52% MR@5, demonstrating true generative capability beyond exact-formula retrieval. Replacing target ED observations with diffraction from non-isomorphic structures of identical composition decreases MR@5 by 22.09 percentage points, confirming that predictions depend on the input diffraction patterns rather than composition alone. ED-CSP and ED-CS establish a benchmark for generative crystal structure prediction from sparse ED observations and provide a foundation for future transfer to experimental data.

</details>


### [170] [Beyond Attention: Signed Integrated Gradients Attribution in a BiomeGPT-Style Microbiome Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06486)
*Oren Nelson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对特征令牌化Transformer（如BiomeGPT）中的可解释性问题，提出使用带符号的、融合感知的Integrated Gradients方法，并结合物种身份保持的源基线（T' = S + A₀），以区分致病与保护性微生物信号，克服[CLS]注意力权重非负且在令牌融合后无法分离输入来源的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对特征令牌化模型（如BiomeGPT）的解释依赖[CLS]注意力权重，但该权重存在两个关键缺陷：一是非负性导致无法区分支持疾病与支持健康的证据方向；二是在令牌融合后计算，遮蔽了物种身份S与丰度A对输出的独立贡献。

Method: 提出使用Integrated Gradients作为带符号且融合感知的归因方法，并设计源派生基线T' = S + A₀，在保留物种身份作为固定生物坐标的同时隔离丰度变化的影响。该方法应用于疾病-健康决策边界，可产生显式区分致病与保护性微生物信号的极性。此外，推荐使用二阶Integrated Hessians以揭示微生物群落成员间的交互规则，即一个成员的扰动如何影响模型对另一成员的敏感度。

Result: 实验表明，该基于梯度的方法能够揭示物种-丰度方向性关系及敏感度诊断，这些信息完全被无符号的[CLS]注意力权重所掩盖。二阶Integrated Hessians可暴露微生物群落交互规则，包括成员间的敏感性影响以及模糊病例的驱动因素。

Conclusion: 本文为BiomeGPT提供了原则性的可解释性方法，且可推广至其他平滑可微的特征令牌化Transformer。代码已开源。

Abstract: In a feature-tokenized transformer (arXiv:2106.11959) such as BiomeGPT (doi:10.64898/2026.01.05.697599), each input token is built by fusing a fixed identity with a sample-specific measurement: a fixed species and a variable abundance, T = S + A. To interpret downstream classification in such models, prior work inspects the attention weights of the special [CLS] token (arXiv:2106.11959, arXiv:1810.04805, BiomeGPT) to rank sample tokens by importance. These weights have two critical limitations: they are nonnegative, so they cannot separate disease-supporting from health-supporting evidence (arXiv:2201.12114), and they act after token fusion, obscuring how the input sources S and A each affect the output.
  To address this we use Integrated Gradients (arXiv:1703.01365), a signed, fusion-aware attribution method, and propose a source-derived baseline T' = S + A_0 for feature-tokenized models such as BiomeGPT, which preserves species identity as a fixed biological coordinate while isolating the effect of abundance variation. Applied to a disease-versus-health decision margin, it yields polarity that explicitly separates pathogenic from protective microbial signals. We show that this gradient-based approach uncovers species-abundance directional relationships and sensitivity diagnostics entirely obscured by unsigned [CLS] attention weights. We further recommend second-order Integrated Hessians (arXiv:2002.04138) to expose microbiome community interaction rules: how a perturbation in one member alters the model's sensitivity to another, and which other species drive ambiguous cases toward disease or health at a given abundance level. This provides a principled approach to explainability in BiomeGPT that generalizes to other smooth and differentiable feature-tokenized transformers. Code is available at https://github.com/nohren/token-source-attribution

</details>


### [171] [Toward Reliable Context Compression for Long-Horizon Agents: An Empirical Study of Execution Instability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06503)
*Guanghui Min,Liang Wu,Mayank Darbari,Chen Chen,Liangjie Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文初步实证研究发现，循环上下文压缩会削弱智能体对最近交互的影响，导致阻塞动作增多、重复探索和运行不稳定。为此提出TRACE框架，通过验证器引导的成对闭环延续来评估每次压缩事件，并利用摘要偏好优化自然语言压缩提示（模型全部冻结）。在AppWorld上的初步结果显示，TRACE在任务性能、多运行可靠性和上下文-执行效率方面优于现有压缩基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程智能体中的循环上下文压缩虽能控制上下文增长，但其行为影响尚未被充分理解。作者观察到压缩会削弱近期交互的影响，导致阻塞动作、重复探索和跨运行不稳定等问题，亟需可靠的压缩评估与优化方法。

Method: 提出TRACE框架，核心思路是：(1) 通过配对闭环延续（从相同环境状态出发）评估每个单独的压缩事件；(2) 利用摘要偏好来优化自然语言压缩提示；(3) 保持所有模型冻结，仅优化提示。

Result: 在AppWorld基准上的初步实验表明，TRACE在任务性能、多运行可靠性和上下文-执行效率方面均优于现有的压缩基线方法。

Conclusion: 研究提供了"边界局部评估"（boundary-local evaluation）作为可靠智能体上下文压缩方向的早期证据，表明通过细粒度、情境化的压缩评估可以实现更可靠的上下文管理。

Abstract: Recurrent context compression controls context growth in long-horizon agents, but its behavioral effects remain poorly understood. In this preliminary empirical study, we show that compression can weaken the influence of recent interactions, increasing blocked actions, repeated exploration, and instability across runs. Motivated by these observations, we introduce TRACE, a verifier-guided framework that evaluates individual compaction events through paired closed-loop continuations from the same environment state and uses summary preferences to optimize a natural-language compression prompt while keeping all models frozen. Initial results on AppWorld show improvements over existing compression baselines in task performance, multi-run reliability, and context--execution efficiency. These findings provide early evidence for boundary-local evaluation as a promising direction for reliable agent context compression.

</details>


### [172] [Unmasking Removal-Budget Confounding: A Matched Operating-Point Evaluation Framework for Adaptive Data Cleaning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06511)
*Wei-Hsiang Chen,Pin-Hsuan Yu,Chen-Hsuan Fang,Jung-Hua Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了自适应数据清洗方法中"移除预算混杂"这一评估偏差——改变划分数会影响决策边界和移除样本总数，导致表面性能提升并非源于真正的污染判别能力。作者提出操作点感知评估框架（匹配预算和匹配召回率对照，辅以AUROC和AUPRC），在多线索自适应清洗器上验证后发现，朴素评估显示的改进在均等化操作点后消失，大部分性能差异在低到中等污染率下缩小或消失，仅在特定低流行率场景和高召回率区域保留真实优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应数据清洗方法用数据驱动的划分取代手动过滤阈值，但改变分组粒度会隐式移动决策边界并改变移除样本总数，造成"移除预算混杂"——看似精度或假阳性率的提升实际上源于更小的移除预算而非更好的污染判别能力，需要一种能消除该偏差的评估方法。

Method: 提出操作点感知评估框架：使用匹配预算和匹配召回率对照，结合阈值无关指标（AUROC和AUPRC）。在重设计的多线索自适应清洗器上进行验证，该设计包含重加权的学习难度线索、辅助欧氏距离线索和增加的分组粒度，用于隔离"干净但困难"样本；并通过假阳性分解分析不同污染率下误差来源。

Result: CIFAR-10和ImageNet-100实验表明：朴素评估下重设计方案的显著性能改进在操作点均等化后消失；假阳性分解显示干净但困难样本在低污染率下主导错误、中等污染率下呈阈值依赖性、重度污染下贡献可忽略；多数性能差异在匹配操作点后缩小或消失，真实排序优势仅在特定低流行率设置和重度污染下的高召回区域保留。

Conclusion: 自适应清洗方法必须在匹配操作点下进行基准测试，才能确保性能提升反映真实的污染判别能力而非移除预算的差异。当前研究结果强调了操作点感知评估的必要性，为自适应数据清洗领域的公平评估提供了方法论指导。

Abstract: Adaptive data-cleaning methods replace manual filtering thresholds with data-driven partitions. However, changing the partition granularity, the number of groups used to segment samples by estimated corruption risk, can implicitly shift the decision boundary and alter the overall number of removed samples. This creates a bias known as removal-budget confounding, where apparent gains in metrics like precision or false-positive rate reflect a smaller removal budget rather than superior corruption discrimination. To address this evaluation bias, we introduce an operating-point-aware evaluation framework that evaluates methods using matched-budget and matched-recall controls alongside threshold-independent metrics (AUROC and AUPRC). We test this framework on a multi-cue adaptive cleaner redesign featuring a reweighted learning-difficulty cue, an auxiliary Euclidean-distance cue, and increased partition granularity intended to isolate clean-but-difficult samples. While naive evaluations (assessing configurations at their own induced operating points) suggest substantial performance improvements for the redesign, these gains disappear once operating points are equalized. False-positive decomposition reveals that clean-but-difficult samples primarily drive error counts at low corruption rates, become threshold-dependent at moderate corruption, and contribute negligibly under severe corruption. Experiments on CIFAR-10 and ImageNet-100 demonstrate that most performance differences observed in naive evaluation shrink or vanish at low-to-moderate corruption when operating points are matched. True ranking advantages only remain in specific low-prevalence settings and in high-recall regions under severe corruption. These findings highlight that adaptive cleaning methods must be benchmarked at matched operating points to ensure performance gains reflect genuine corruption discrimination.

</details>


### [173] [Target-Weighted Neyman Allocation: Experimental Design for Heterogeneous Treatment Effects under Population Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06512)
*Hoang Dang,Luan Pham,Minh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TWNA（Target-Weighted Neyman Allocation），一种两阶段分层实验设计方法，通过利用试点估计的组-臂结果方差来分配最终阶段样本量和处理概率，以优化目标加权组平均处理效应（GATE）的估计精度。该方法在部署重要性与统计难度之间取得平衡，并扩展到处理部署组成不确定性的场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际应用中，随机实验通常在一个人群中进行，但结果用于指导另一个人群的决策。按实验比例分配会浪费预算在不常出现的组上，而按部署比例分配又会低估难以精确测量的组。现有方法无法同时兼顾部署重要性与统计测量难度。

Method: 提出TWNA两阶段分层设计：第一阶段通过试点估计各分组-处理臂的结果方差，第二阶段据此分配样本量和处理概率。由此得到Oracle规则的闭式解，并提供了plug-in规则在试点方差估计趋于稳定时恢复Oracle性质的保证。同时扩展到处理部署组成不确定性（从大致已知到完全未知）的情况，并区分权重鲁棒性与针对偏态、稀有事件或污染结果的试点鲁棒变体。

Result: 模拟实验和真实协变量基准测试表明，当分组既具有部署重要性又难以测量时，TWNA带来的增益最大。

Conclusion: TWNA通过两阶段设计有效平衡了部署重要性与统计测量困难的权衡，为跨人群决策场景下的实验分配提供了更优方案，并能适应部署组成的不确定性。

Abstract: Randomized experiments are often run in one population to guide decisions in another. Allocating by experimental proportions wastes budget on groups that rarely appear in deployment, whereas allocating by deployment proportions under-samples groups that are hard to measure precisely. We propose \textbf{TWNA} (Target-Weighted Neyman Allocation), a two-stage stratified design that uses pilot estimates of group--arm outcome variances to allocate final-stage sample sizes and treatment probabilities for target-weighted group average treatment effect (GATE) precision. The oracle rule has a closed form and balances deployment importance with statistical difficulty; the plug-in rule recovers it as pilot variance estimates stabilize. We also extend TWNA to handle uncertainty about deployment composition, remaining robust whether the target mix is roughly known or entirely unknown. Finally, we distinguish this weight robustness from a pilot-robust variant for skewed, rare-event, or contaminated outcomes. Simulations and real-covariate benchmarks show the largest gains when groups are both deployment-important and difficult to measure.

</details>


### [174] [CertBind from Multimodal Connectivity to Certifiable Retrieval Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06516)
*Shuheng Cao,Zhenhao Zhang,Ruiqi Chen,Renjie Cao,Weijia Zhang,Siyu Zhang,Jiaxin Liu,Xiangyu Zeng,Haotian Geng,Fan Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CertBind是一个多尺度理论框架，用于为冻结的多模态编码器连接图提供可认证的组合能力。它在节点、边、路径和查询四个尺度上分别建立任务识别边界、图级错误控制、恢复半径和点证书，使系统能区分Direct、Recovery、Certified和Abstain四种状态。实验表明该方法在保持无伤害的同时有效恢复了原生检索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻量级连接器虽然在表征层面使冻结的多模态编码器可组合，但在任务决策层面存在一个问题：连接路径可能扩展跨模态覆盖范围的同时，改变甚至破坏已有的原生检索能力。现有方法缺乏对这种组合行为进行系统化、可证明的保证。

Method: CertBind提出多尺度可认证组合理论：在节点尺度，用原生锚点建立精确的任务识别边界；在边尺度，用契约感知的保形排序提供图级族错误控制；在路径尺度，用重叠感知的预算和干净校准在声明条件下产生有限样本恢复半径；在查询尺度，该半径生成覆盖的top-k候选集，当大小等于k时成为点证书。系统据此将路由分类为Direct、Recovery、Certified和Abstain四种状态。

Result: 在C-MCR共享路由上，原生CLIP R@1从0.524降至0.290。生产回退恢复了0.963±0.002的干净检索性能，通过分支记录了1.000的无伤害值。

Conclusion: CertBind将多模态组合能力从可连接的表征扩展到了可认证的任务决策层面，为组合式多模态系统提供了理论保证和实际可用的质量控制机制。

Abstract: Lightweight connectors make frozen multimodal encoders composable at the representation level. Deployment exposes a second problem at the level of task decisions. A connected route can expand cross-modal reach while changing an established native retrieval capability. We introduce CertBind, a multiscale theory of certifiable composition for frozen multimodal connector graphs. At the node scale, native anchors establish the exact task identification boundary under the stated chart model. At the edge scale, contract-aware conformal ranks provide graph-wide family-wise error control. At the path scale, an overlap-aware budget and clean calibration yield a finite-sample recovery radius under declared conditions. At the query scale, this radius yields a covered top-k candidate set that becomes a point certificate when its size equals k. CertBind therefore retains supported routes as Direct, sends only flagged routes to recovery, returns Certified for decisive recovery, and returns Abstain for unresolved queries. The evaluated C-MCR shared route reduced native CLIP R@1 from 0.524 to 0.290. The production fallback recovered 0.963 +- 0.002 of clean retrieval, while the passing branch recorded a no-harm value of 1.000. CertBind extends multimodal composability from connected representations to certifiable task decisions.

</details>


### [175] [Online Security Learning in Cooperative Multi-Agent Systems under Hidden Byzantine Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06520)
*Ximing Sun,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究多智能体系统在拜占庭攻击下的在线协同控制问题。攻击者可以悄悄覆盖团队联合行动中的自身坐标，学习者仅能观察到计划行动、公开奖励和公开状态。作者刻画了攻击者信息量对问题几何结构的影响，给出了安全学习的理论极限，并提出了基于阶段绑定的鲁棒估计-决策学习器，实现了 ~O(H²S√AK)+E[D_K] 的安全遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多智能体协同控制系统中，部分智能体可能被拜占庭攻击者劫持并秘密篡改团队计划行动，这严重威胁系统安全。现有研究缺乏对这一场景下安全学习问题的系统性理论分析，尤其是攻击者观测信息对问题结构的影响、安全学习的理论极限以及有效算法的设计。

Method: 作者首先通过分析攻击者信息量来刻画问题几何：可观测计划行动的攻击者诱导出精确的 (s,a)-矩形鲁棒 MDP（行由覆盖诱导的公开结果律的凸包构成），而盲目攻击者诱导出 s-矩形模型。随后通过两个不可区分的时间步长-1实例证明安全遗憾下界为 Ω(K)。最后提出阶段绑定的鲁棒估计到决策学习器，利用对抗性覆盖的结构特性进行估计和决策。

Result: 证明了安全遗憾可精确分解为返回遗憾与累积响应差距 D_K 之和；给出了 Ω(K) 的期望安全遗憾下界（即使返回遗憾为零）；提出的学习器达到 ~O(H²S√AK)+E[D_K] 的安全遗憾上界。

Conclusion: 该研究为拜占庭攻击下可靠多智能体系统提供了全面的理论基础和算法基础，明确了攻击者信息量决定问题几何结构这一关键洞察，并验证了安全学习中 D_K 依赖的不可避免性。

Abstract: We study online cooperative control of a multi-agent system under Byzantine attacks. Namely, an unknown, fixed subset of agents are Byzantine comprised and can stealthily overwrite its own coordinates of the team's planned joint action after observing that plan. The learner observes planned actions, public rewards, and public states, but neither the overwrite nor the executed joint action. Our objective is security: to optimize the team performance against the worst overwrites and achieve the optimal security value. We first show that the attacker's information determines the geometry. An attacker that observes the planned action induces an exact $(s,a)$-rectangular robust Markov decision process (MDP) whose rows are convex hulls of overwrite-induced public-outcome laws, whereas a blind attacker induces an $s$-rectangular model. We then identify the information-theoretic limit of security learning, showing that the security regret decomposes exactly into return regret against the response generating the data and a cumulative response gap $D_K$. Two indistinguishable horizon-one instances force $Ω(K)$ expected security regret while return regret is zero, showing that dependence on $D_K$ is unavoidable. Finally, we develop a stage-tied robust estimation-to-decisions learner and prove a regret bound of $\widetilde{\mathcal O}\!\left(H^2S\sqrt{AK}\right)+\mathbb E[D_K]$. Our studies thus provide comprehensive theoretical and algorithmic foundations of reliable multi-agent systems under Byzantine attacks.

</details>


### [176] [Robust Average-Reward Markov Decision Processes: Minimax-Optimal Learning via Plug-in Reductions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06545)
*Yuepeng Yang,Yuxin Chen,Yuejie Chi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在平均奖励准则下，分布鲁棒马尔可夫决策过程（RMDP）中学习ε最优鲁棒策略所需的样本复杂度。作者确定了扰动尺度σH₀在高容忍与低容忍区间之间的分界，并给出了匹配的上下界，证明了极小极大总样本复杂度为NSA，包含线性跨度项和鲁棒性特有的二次项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布鲁棒MDP为模型不确定性下的序贯决策提供了原则性框架，但此前缺乏在平均奖励准则下学习鲁棒策略所需样本数量的精确刻画。理解样本复杂度对于实际应用中权衡数据收集成本与决策质量至关重要。

Method: 作者采用基于约简的插件式方法，在已知跨度参数的情况下设计跨度感知程序，以及在未知跨度参数时设计跨度无关程序来自数据校准约简类型（名义或鲁棒）及其折扣因子。通过识别扰动尺度σH₀作为高容忍与低容忍区间的分界，分别分析两种情况下的样本复杂度。

Result: 作者给出了匹配的上下界，证明极小极大总样本复杂度为NSA，在ε≳σH₀时约为SA·min{H₀,Hσ}/ε²，在ε≲σH₀时约为SA·(min{H₀,Hσ}+σHσ²)/ε²。复杂度由类似名义AMDP结果的线性跨度项和仅在低容忍区间出现的鲁棒性特有项组成。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了平均奖励准则下分布鲁棒MDP的样本复杂度，揭示了高容忍与低容忍区间在复杂度结构上的本质差异。鲁棒性特有项仅出现在低容忍区间，表明在不确定性容忍度较低时需要额外样本以应对模型不确定性。

Abstract: Distributionally robust Markov decision processes provide a principled framework for sequential decision making under model uncertainty. We study how many samples are necessary and sufficient to learn an $\varepsilon$-optimal robust policy under the average-reward criterion. A generative model provides samples from the nominal transition kernel, whereas policy performance is evaluated over $(s,a)$-rectangular total-variation uncertainty sets of radius at most $σ$.
  Let $H_0$ and $H_σ$ denote the nominal and robust optimal bias spans, respectively. We identify $σH_0$ as the perturbation scale separating high- and low-tolerance regimes. Our matching upper and lower bounds show that, up to logarithmic factors, the minimax total sample complexity is $$ NSA \asymp \frac{SA}{\varepsilon^2}\begin{cases} \min\{H_0,H_σ\}, & \varepsilon\gtrsimσH_0,\\ \min\{H_0,H_σ\}+σH_σ^2, & \varepsilon\lesssimσH_0. \end{cases} $$ Here $S$ and $A$ are the numbers of states and actions, and $N$ is the number of samples per state-action pair. The sample complexity consists of a linear-span term that resembles the nominal AMDP results and a robustness-specific term that appears only in the low-tolerance regime. We attain these rates using reduction-based plug-in procedures that select the reduction---nominal or robust---and its discount factor: a span-informed procedure that makes these choices using known span parameters, and a span-agnostic procedure that calibrates both choices from data.

</details>


### [177] [Newton-Schulz Retraction-Based Inference Enables Hidden Quantum Markov Models to Outperform Classical HMMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06554)
*Ning Ning*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NS-RIS算法，一种基于Newton-Schulz收缩在Stiefel流形上进行推断的可扩展学习算法，用于训练保迹隐藏量子马尔可夫模型（HQMM）。这是首个在非量子生成数据上显著优于EM训练HMM的HQMM方法，并在多类基准上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HMM是广泛使用的序列数据概率模型，但隐藏动态复杂时能力受限。HQMM通过密度矩阵和量子操作泛化HMM，理论上表达力更强，但现有HQMM学习方法在非量子生成数据上未能一致超越EM训练的HMM，限制了其实用价值。

Method: NS-RIS利用Newton-Schulz正交化计算极因子搜索方向，同时保持Stiefel流形可行性，避免昂贵的矩阵分解。算法在光滑性、随机梯度和有限Newton-Schulz精度等标准假设下建立了有限时间平稳性保证。

Result: 在合成HMM基准上，NS-RIS平均超出EM和SOTA方法COSM 38.5%，最高达50.6%；在合成HQMM基准上，测试指标较COSM提升18.9%，运行时间减少12.0%；在真实Splice分类基准上，隐维度6和8下分类误差分别较COSM降低17.9%和14.9%。

Conclusion: 结果表明HQMM不再只是HMM的理论推广，而是对科学序列数据实用且表现力强的模型。NS-RIS首次证明HQMM能在非量子生成数据上显著超越EM训练的HMM。

Abstract: Hidden Markov models (HMMs) are widely used probabilistic models for discrete sequential data but can be limited when hidden dynamics are complex. Hidden quantum Markov models (HQMMs) generalize HMMs by replacing probability vectors with density matrices and stochastic transitions with quantum operations, enabling richer latent representations. However, existing HQMM learning methods have not consistently outperformed Expectation--Maximization (EM)-trained HMMs on data not generated by quantum processes, limiting their practical applicability. We introduce NS-RIS, Newton--Schulz Retraction-based Inference on the Stiefel manifold, a scalable algorithm for learning trace-preserving HQMMs. NS-RIS uses Newton--Schulz orthogonalization to compute a polar-factor search direction while preserving Stiefel-manifold feasibility, avoiding costly matrix decompositions. We further establish a finite-time stationarity guarantee under standard assumptions on smoothness, stochastic gradients, and finite Newton--Schulz accuracy. Empirically, NS-RIS provides the first benchmark evidence that an HQMM can significantly outperform an EM-trained HMM on data not generated by a quantum model. On synthetic HMM-generated benchmarks, NS-RIS outperforms both EM and the state-of-the-art HQMM method COSM, improving the evaluation metric by an average of 38.5% and by up to 50.6%. On a synthetic HQMM benchmark, it improves the test metric over COSM by 18.9% while reducing runtime by 12.0%. On the real-world Splice classification benchmark, NS-RIS also surpasses both EM and COSM in higher-dimensional latent regimes, reducing mean classification error by 17.9% for latent dimension 6 and 14.9% for latent dimension 8 relative to COSM. These results move HQMMs beyond a theoretical generalization of HMMs and establish them as practical and expressive models for scientific sequence data.

</details>


### [178] [Bootstrap-Conditioned Action Selection with Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06559)
*Devansh Gupta,Shiv Tavker,Dmitry Efimov,Suchitra Sathyanarayana,Gitanjali Bhutani,Boris N. Oreshkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BC-ICL方法，利用预训练表格基础模型的上下文学习能力，通过bootstrap重采样交互历史来构建随机化策略，用于在线上下文赌博机决策，在标准基准上取得了优于既有基线的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文赌博机在个性化任务中面临稀疏有偏交互数据、不确定性估计不可靠以及严重冷启动等实际部署难题，现有方法难以有效应对。

Method: 提出BC-ICL策略：每轮对交互历史进行bootstrap重采样，将冻结的预训练ICL模型基于该重采样结果进行条件化处理，对所有动作打分，并选择得分最高的动作。同时引入arm-context条件架构，促进跨动作的统计强度共享，避免孤立臂赌博机中常见的bootstrap失败模式。

Result: 在标准上下文赌博机基准套件上，BC-ICL在严格在线协议下取得了强早期后悔度和整体后悔度表现，优于既有基线方法。

Conclusion: 预训练表格基础模型结合ICL可以被有效转化为在线决策的随机化策略，为实际部署中的稀疏数据、冷启动和不确定性估计问题提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Contextual bandits offer a natural framework for sample-efficient personalization, but practical deployment remains difficult under sparse, biased interaction data, unreliable uncertainty estimates, and severe cold starts. We study whether pre-trained tabular foundation models with in-context learning can be turned into randomized policies for online decision making. We propose BC-ICL (Bootstrap-conditioned action selection using ICL), which at each round draws a bootstrap resample of the interaction history, conditions a frozen pre-trained ICL model on that resample, scores all actions, and selects the action with the highest sampled score. We further introduce an arm-context conditioning architecture that promotes shared statistical strength across actions and helps avoid common bootstrap failure modes of isolated-arm bandits. Empirically, this policy delivers strong early-round regret and regret performance on standard contextual bandit suites, outperforming established baselines under a strict online protocol.

</details>


### [179] [Theoretical Foundations of Communication-Efficient, Robust, and Practical Distributed and Federated Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06563)
*Grigory Malinovsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文围绕联邦学习和分布式优化中的七个关键挑战展开研究，提出了ProxSkip、方差缩减ProxSkip、部分客户端参与下的本地梯度加速、服务端步长与无放回采样、随机重排中的梯度差分压缩、拜占庭鲁棒性与部分参与兼容方案，以及基于随机非对称链的低秩适配理论框架，均提供了理论保证和实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习和优化相互促进，现代大规模训练依赖经典优化原理，但分布式系统的约束使得这些基础需要重新审视。本文旨在弥合理论与实践之间的鸿沟，解决联邦学习和分布式优化中的关键瓶颈。

Method: 论文通过七项研究系统性地提出新算法和理论框架：1) ProxSkip证明本地梯度步可加速通信；2) 方差缩减ProxSkip消除随机局部更新的邻域误差；3) 验证部分客户端参与下本地步骤仍保持通信加速；4) 证明服务端步长和无放回采样在异构场景下改善收敛；5) 随机重排中压缩梯度差分优于压缩梯度本身；6) 利用梯度差分裁剪同时实现拜占庭鲁棒性和部分参与；7) 建立基于随机非对称链的低秩适配首个理论框架。

Result: 七项研究均获得了理论与实践的成果：本地梯度加速通信有严格理论依据；方差缩减方法消除邻域误差；部分参与不影响通信加速；异构场景收敛性得到改进；梯度差分压缩在理论和实践上均更优；拜占庭鲁棒性与部分参与可同时实现；低秩适配有了首个理论框架。所有理论结果均通过数值实验验证。

Conclusion: 本文在联邦学习和分布式优化的理论与实验结合方面做出了全面贡献，提出了新颖的算法框架，在现实假设下建立了精确的收敛保证，并通过数值实验验证了理论分析，为大规模模型训练和微调提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Machine learning and optimization have advanced together, with practical demands motivating new theory and theoretical breakthroughs enabling new applications. Modern large-scale training relies on classical optimization principles, but the constraints of distributed systems require these foundations to be reconsidered. This thesis addresses seven challenges at the intersection of theory and practice, focusing on key bottlenecks in federated learning and distributed optimization. First, we introduce ProxSkip and prove that local gradient steps can accelerate communication, providing a theoretical foundation for this widely used heuristic. Second, we develop Variance Reduced ProxSkip, which eliminates the neighborhood error of stochastic local updates while balancing communication and local computation. Third, we show that local steps retain their communication acceleration under partial client participation. Fourth, we prove that server-side stepsizes and sampling without replacement improve convergence in heterogeneous settings. Fifth, for Random Reshuffling, we demonstrate that compressing gradient differences rather than gradients yields better theoretical and practical performance. Sixth, we establish that Byzantine robustness and partial participation can be achieved simultaneously using gradient-difference clipping. Finally, we develop the first theoretical framework for low-rank adaptation based on randomized asymmetric chains, providing new insights into fine-tuning large models. Across these contributions, we introduce novel algorithmic frameworks, establish sharp guarantees under realistic assumptions, and support the theory with numerical experiments.

</details>


### [180] [Quantization Damage Is Multiplicative, Not Additive](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06564)
*Zekun Wu,Swati Dhiman,Adriano Koshiyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现量化大语言模型并非引入固定大小的噪声，而是对决策边际进行收缩（margin shrinkage），且收缩因子随比特数降低而崩溃（4比特中位数0.86，3比特0.33，2比特0.00）。这种收缩会导致工具调用、安全拒绝等关键决策静默失效，即使基准分数几乎不变。作者提出了一种基于成对边际的预测方法，可校准地估计每个决策的翻转概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化是LLM实际部署的方式，但低于4比特会损伤模型，现有方法无法指出哪些具体决策会改变。先前的假设认为量化添加固定大小的噪声，这使得高置信度决策被认为是安全的，但实际中量化损伤是"静默"的：压缩后的智能体停止调用工具、失去一半安全拒绝，而基准分数几乎不变。

Method: 作者定义了二选一决策的边际（margin）=模型所选选项得分减去最佳替代选项得分，并在量化前后追踪该边际。实验覆盖16个模型、8个模型家族、3种量化方法和8到2比特的位宽。通过统计拟合和成对边际集合来估计每个决策的翻转概率，而非整体性能指标。

Result: 量化不增加固定大小的噪声，而是乘以一个随位宽崩溃的收缩因子；该收缩减少了大幅边际提供的保护，模型自身的小偏差决定失败方向（3比特时工具调用决策朝不行动坍塌，而选择哪个工具不受影响）。加性噪声模型在受损的工具和安全决策上从不胜出。拟合关系在未参与拟合的决策上预测翻转率的中位数误差仅1.8个百分点，期望校准误差0.004（131,758个预测）。

Conclusion: 相同的边际收缩形式在所有被测模型中成立，但每个模型的常数各不相同、不可迁移。通过每个模型和位宽测量的成对边际集，可以估计哪些决策会失效而无需完整生成式评估；在成本匹配测试下，增加一个比特是比任何修复手段都更廉价的方案。

Abstract: Quantization is how large language models are actually deployed, and below four bits it is known to hurt. What nobody can say is which of the model's decisions will change at a given bit-width. The damage is silent: a compressed agent stops calling its tools, then loses half its safety refusals, yet benchmark scores barely move. Prior work assumes quantization adds noise of a roughly fixed size, which would make confident decisions safe. We measure the decision itself instead. The margin of a two-way decision is the model's score for the option it picks minus the score of its best alternative; we track it before and after quantization across 16 models from 8 model families, three quantization methods, and bit-widths from 8 down to 2. Quantization does not add fixed-size noise to the margin. It multiplies the margin by a factor that collapses with bit-width (median 0.86 at 4 bits, 0.33 at 3, 0.00 at 2); we call this margin shrinkage. This contraction reduces the protection a large margin affords; the model's own small biases pick the direction of failure: at 3 bits the decision to call a tool collapses toward inaction while the choice of which tool is untouched. In fitted statistical comparison, additive-noise accounts never win on the damaged tool and safety decisions. The fitted relation predicts flip rates within a median of 1.8 percentage points on held-out decisions, though no flip was used in the fit; per decision, the predicted flip probabilities are calibrated uncertainty estimates (expected calibration error 0.004 over 131,758 predictions). The same form holds in every model we measure, but the constants are each model's own and do not transfer. A small paired margin set, measured per model and bit-width, estimates which decisions break without full generative evaluation; under our cost-matched tests, nothing repairs damage more cheaply than one more bit.

</details>


### [181] [CrystalGRPO: Target-Aligned and Coverage-Preserving Reinforcement Learning for Flow-Based Crystal Structure Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06582)
*Kaixiang Su,Hongfei Xue,Qiang Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CrystalGRPO是一个面向晶体结构预测的强化学习后训练框架，通过将ODE-to-SDE策略扩展至联合坐标-晶格状态，结合MACE能量预测与StructureMatcher恢复评分，提供Q和C两种模式，分别优化单次采样恢复和有限预算下的目标恢复，在MP-20和MPTS-52数据集上均优于纯坐标强化基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于流的生成模型在CSP中能高效产生候选结构，但其预训练目标未直接优化下游目标恢复。现有强化学习后训练方法主要依赖能量奖励和仅坐标的随机策略，存在两个缺陷：预测能量无法识别参考多晶型，且奖励驱动的集中化会降低Top-N恢复所需的候选覆盖度。

Method: CrystalGRPO将现有的ODE-to-SDE策略构建扩展到联合坐标-晶格状态空间，结合MACE预测能量与StructureMatcher恢复评分作为奖励，并提供两种操作模式：CrystalGRPO-Q优先单次采样恢复，CrystalGRPO-C通过全轨迹参考正则化与覆盖感知的组优势相结合，保证有限预算下的目标恢复能力。

Result: 在MP-20和MPTS-52数据集上，分别以PXRDGen和OMatG为骨干网络，两种变体在所有四种骨干-数据集组合下，相对纯坐标强化学习使单样本和二十样本RMSE均降低。CrystalGRPO-Q持续提升Top-1性能，而CrystalGRPO-C在所有设置下获得更高的Top-20。

Conclusion: CrystalGRPO通过联合坐标-晶格策略和覆盖感知的组优势设计，有效解决了现有强化后训练中能量奖励不可靠与候选覆盖度下降的问题，在不同骨干网络和数据规模下展现出稳定的性能提升，为CSP后训练提供了更优的范式。

Abstract: Flow-based generative models can efficiently produce candidate structures for crystal structure prediction (CSP), but their pretrained objectives do not directly optimize downstream target recovery. Reinforcement-learning post-training offers a flexible solution, yet existing approaches rely primarily on energy rewards and coordinate-only stochastic policies. Predicted energy does not identify the reference polymorph, while reward-driven concentration can reduce the candidate coverage required for Top-N recovery. We introduce CrystalGRPO, a CSP-aligned post-training framework that extends existing ODE-to-SDE policy constructions to the joint coordinate--lattice state. CrystalGRPO combines MACE-predicted energy with a StructureMatcher-based recovery score and provides two operating modes: CrystalGRPO-Q, which prioritizes single-draw recovery, and CrystalGRPO-C, which combines full-trajectory reference regularization with a coverage-aware group advantage to preserve finite-budget target recovery. Across MP-20 and MPTS-52 with PXRDGen and OMatG backbones, both variants reduce one- and twenty-sample RMSE relative to coordinate-only reinforcement in all four backbone--dataset settings. CrystalGRPO-Q consistently improves Top-1, whereas CrystalGRPO-C achieves a higher Top-20 across all settings.

</details>


### [182] [Flowing Through States: Neural ODE Regularization for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06595)
*Mohamed Ghanem,Bernd Finkbeiner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于神经ODE的正则化方法，通过显式建模潜在状态转移的动态过程，使强化学习智能体的潜在表征与环境真实动态保持一致，在Atari基准（A2C）和网格世界（PPO）上取得了显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经网络在处理序列决策任务时，虽然环境动力学决定了语义状态的演化，但潜在状态的转移通常是隐式的，导致潜在表征与环境动态之间可能出现不一致。这种错位会损害智能体的学习效果。

Method: 将MDP轨迹与ODE流进行类比（两者中当前状态完全决定后续状态），在此基础上引入基于神经ODE的正则化方法，强制潜在嵌入遵循一致的ODE流，从而将表征学习与环境动态对齐。该方法可广泛适用于深度学习智能体，论文将其集成到Actor-Critic算法中验证有效性。

Result: 将方法集成到A2C和PPO中后，在多个标准Atari基准（A2C）和网格世界环境（PPO）上均取得了显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模潜在动态并施加神经ODE正则化，可以有效对齐表征学习与环境动态，为强化学习等序列决策任务提供了一种通用且有效的改进方案。

Abstract: Neural networks applied to sequential decision-making tasks typically rely on latent representations of environment states. While environment dynamics dictate how semantic states evolve, the corresponding latent transitions are usually left implicit, creating a potential misalignment between the two. We propose to model latent dynamics explicitly by drawing an analogy between Markov decision process (MDP) trajectories and ordinary differential equation (ODE) flows: in both cases, the current state fully determines its successors. Building on this view, we introduce a neural ODE-based regularization method that enforces latent embeddings to follow consistent ODE flows, thereby aligning representation learning with environment dynamics. Although broadly applicable to deep learning agents, we demonstrate its effectiveness in reinforcement learning by integrating it into Actor-Critic algorithms. Our approach yields major performance gains across various standard Atari benchmarks for A2C and gridworld environments for PPO.

</details>


### [183] [Retrofitting Linear Attention into Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06628)
*Jinha Kim,Younghun Roh,Jaeyeon Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出块混合注意力（block-hybrid attention），在扩散语言模型（dLLM）中保留当前去噪块内的精确softmax注意力，同时对先前块使用线性注意力，从而消除前缀注意力开销。通过最小化后训练（约60小时）将LLaDA 2.1（16B）中20个注意力层中的6层替换为混合注意力，在保持基准性能的同时实现最高1.7倍解码吞吐量提升，并支持更多并发请求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型虽能通过并行解码加速推理，但即便使用KV缓存，每个去噪步骤仍需关注所有先前块，反复产生前缀注意力成本，成为推理瓶颈。

Method: 提出块混合注意力：活动去噪块内保留精确softmax注意力，对先前块应用线性注意力；基于LoLCAT方法将预训练dLLM（LLaDA 2.1 16B）中6/20个注意力层替换为混合注意力，并进行约60小时的最小程度后训练。

Result: 在HumanEval上72.0% vs 75.6%，MBPP+上63.0% vs 57.7%，CMATH上86.7% vs 88.3%；Triton实现达到最高1.7倍解码吞吐量提升，并支持更多并发请求直到内存耗尽。

Conclusion: 实验证明预训练dLLM可以通过块混合注意力高效线性化以加速推理，且只需极小的性能代价和后训练成本。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (dLLMs) offer a promising alternative to autoregressive models by accelerating inference through parallel decoding. Recent dLLMs commonly use blockwise semi-autoregressive decoding, generating blocks autoregressively while denoising tokens within each active block in parallel. However, despite KV caching, each denoising step still attends to all previous blocks, repeatedly incurring prefix-attention cost. Motivated by this bottleneck, we ask whether dLLM inference can be further accelerated by linearizing attention over previous blocks. We introduce block-hybrid attention, which retains exact softmax attention within the active denoising block while applying linear attention over previous blocks. We show that this hybrid attention can be retrofitted into a pretrained dLLM with minimal post-training: LLaDA-Hybrid replaces 6 of the 20 attention layers in LLaDA~2.1, a 16B open-source dLLM, largely following LoLCAT (Zhang et al, 2024). The conversion takes only approximately 60 hours while preserving benchmark performance: 72.0% vs. 75.6% on HumanEval, 63.0% vs. 57.7% on MBPP+, and 86.7% vs. 88.3% on CMATH. With a Triton implementation, LLaDA-Hybrid achieves up to $1.7\times$ higher decoding throughput and supports more concurrent requests before exhausting memory, showing that pretrained dLLMs can be efficiently linearized for faster inference. Our code is available at: https://github.com/Diuven/LLaDA-Hybrid.

</details>


### [184] [The Sparsity Whisperer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06630)
*Linghao Kong,Inimai Subramanian,Micah Adler,Dan Alistarh,Dan Gutfreund,Nir Shavit*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于"输出差异保持"原理的剪枝方法家族（Wisp、Wisp+、Whisper），强调LLM剪枝时不仅要保留激活值，更应保留神经元对相似输入产生不同输出的能力。在Llama 2和3.1系列（7B-405B）上超越Wanda和SparseGPT等基线，并可增强RIA、ALPS等更强方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有剪枝标准主要关注保留大激活值或重构层输出，忽略了MLP up/gate投影中对稀疏性敏感的神经元执行的关键计算——将相似输入分离为不同输出，因此需要新的剪枝准则来保留输出差异。

Method: 提出三类基于差异信息的剪枝方法：1) Wisp——一阶无更新方法，使用输入差异范数对权重打分；2) Wisp+——在Wisp基础上按神经元级利用每个神经元最强分离的输入对来细化得分；3) Whisper——二阶方法，使用轻正则化的差异Hessian作为重构目标。

Result: 在Llama 2和3.1（7B-405B）上，Whisper一致优于强重构基线；无更新变体在受限场景下优于激活感知基线；在结构化稀疏、下游评估和其他模型家族上均超越Wanda和SparseGPT；与RIA、ALPS结合可进一步扩展精度-效率前沿。

Conclusion: 保留输出差异是LLM后训练稀疏化中广泛有用且可组合的信号，可以极低额外成本改善整体精度-运行时间权衡。

Abstract: Pruning reduces the inference cost of large language models, but existing criteria primarily preserve large activations or reconstruct layer outputs. We argue that this overlooks a key computation performed by particularly sparsity-sensitive neurons in the MLP up and gate projections: separating similar inputs into dissimilar outputs. This suggests that effective pruning should preserve not only activations, but also the differences between outputs more broadly. We introduce a family of difference-informed pruning methods built upon this principle. Wisp is a first-order, update-free method that scores weights using input-difference norms, and Wisp+ refines this score neuronwise using the input pairs each neuron separates most strongly. Finally, Whisper is a second-order method that uses a lightly regularized difference Hessian as its reconstruction objective. Across Llama 2 and 3.1 models from 7B to 405B parameters, our second-order variant consistently improves over strong reconstruction-based baselines, while our update-free variants improve over activation-aware baselines, especially in constrained settings. The improvements over Wanda and SparseGPT extend to structured sparsity, downstream evaluations, and other model families. Augmenting stronger techniques such as RIA and ALPS with our difference-informed criteria yields further improvements, shifting the overall accuracy-runtime frontier outward at negligible additional cost. These results suggest that preserving output differences is a broadly useful and composable signal for post-training LLM sparsification.

</details>


### [185] [Cryptanalytic Extraction of Isolated Bias-Free GLU Feed-Forward Blocks by Antipodal Separation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06631)
*Chunhui Shi,Xinwen Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对无偏置GLU（门控线性单元）前馈块的前向查询恢复方法，通过有限差分曲率获取门控方向候选，利用x和-x的成对观测分离门控幅度、方向与值分支耦合。在多种模型（Qwen、Llama、Gemma）上达到亚百分比中位验证误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有密码分析提取方法针对ReLU网络、GELU/SiLU等逐分量激活网络以及Transformer最终投影矩阵，但无法恢复现代语言模型中广泛使用的无偏置GLU前馈块，因其具有两分支结构（激活的线性投影与第二个学习的线性投影逐隐层单元相乘），不同于已有方法处理的网络类别。

Method: 提出一种构造性的多阶段前向查询恢复原语：1）利用有限差分曲率提供门控方向候选；2）通过x与-x处的成对观测来分离门控幅度、方向及值分支耦合。整个过程针对隔离的无偏置GLU块设计。

Result: 在高精度目标上，六个Qwen层、8,192单元的Llama子问题以及全维度Gemma块均达到亚百分比中位验证误差；四个有限精度配置保持在5%中位误差以下，但均未能完整复现所有存储权重。该方法是孤立块级实验，尚未实现端到端模型API攻击，因为从最终模型输出推导所需内部块响应的问题仍未解决。

Conclusion: 本文成功实现了对隔离GLU块的权重提取，填补了现有密码分析方法无法处理两分支GLU结构的空白，但距离实际端到端攻击仍有关键障碍——从模型最终输出中恢复内部块响应的难题有待解决。

Abstract: Cryptanalytic extraction has been demonstrated for ReLU networks, for networks using componentwise activations such as GELU or SiLU, and for a Transformer's final projection matrix. These methods do not recover the bias-free Gated Linear Unit (GLU) feed-forward blocks used in many modern language models. Such a block multiplies an activated linear projection by a second learned linear projection within each hidden unit, a two-branch structure absent from the network classes and final-layer setting addressed by those methods. We give a constructive, multi-stage forward-query recovery primitive for isolated bias-free GLU blocks. Finite-difference curvature supplies gate-direction candidates, and paired observations at x and -x separate gate magnitude, orientation, and value-branch coupling. Across high-precision targets, six Qwen layers, an 8,192-unit Llama subproblem, and a full-dimensional Gemma block all reach sub-percent median validation error. Four finite-precision configurations remain below 5 percent median error, but none reproduces every stored weight. These isolated-block experiments are not an end-to-end model-API attack: deriving the required internal block responses from final model outputs remains unsolved.

</details>


### [186] [Bypassing Krum: Selection-Aware Backdoor Attacks in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06637)
*Srinivasan Subramanian,Md. Abdullah Al Hafiz Khan,Kazi Aminul Islam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Krum-Proxy攻击，一种针对联邦学习中基于距离的鲁棒聚合方法（如Krum和Multi-Krum）的选择感知后门注入策略。通过两阶段优化过程，该方法构造既能迷惑距离聚合器又保持隐蔽性的恶意更新，实验证明其比现有攻击方法具有更高的攻击成功率，同时保持干净精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的距离基于聚合规则（如Krum、Multi-Krum）假设良性更新形成紧凑簇，但其几何特性可被自适应攻击者利用。目前缺乏能够主动优化恶意更新以渗透良性分布密集核心的有效攻击方法。

Method: 提出Krum-Proxy攻击方法：(1)两阶段优化流程，分离任务特定攻击目标与几何感知精化；(2)使用最近邻代理、随机参考建模和锚点引导对齐；(3)引入投影机制，将对抗更新约束在现实的范数和方差界限内以保持隐蔽性。

Result: 在标准联邦学习基准上的实验表明，Krum-Proxy实现了更高的攻击成功率，同时保持了干净精度，证明了距离基于聚合方法对选择感知对手的脆弱性。

Conclusion: 距离基于的鲁棒聚合方法无法抵御选择感知的自适应攻击，需要开发更强大的聚合策略来应对此类威胁。
